Later 2:153-157 confirmed the loop under stress: restraint, prayer, loss, declared return, and answering mercy make gratitude perceptible as sustained orientation.

## 2:153 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ ٱسْتَعِينُوا۟ بِٱلصَّبْرِ وَٱلصَّلَوٰةِ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ مَعَ ٱلصَّٰبِرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Restraint and prayer create an accompaniment structure under pressure.** `ع و ن B001` supplies backing, `ص ب ر B001` restraint from panic and `ص ب ر B003` bearing surety or standing with someone, while `ص ل و B006` supplies following in second place. The command follows reciprocal remembrance and precedes killing, partial loss, and affliction (2:154-156); its close explicitly places God “with” the restrainers. The packet supplies support, surety, and coming trials; I infer that disciplined holding opens experienced accompaniment. Patience reads as relational load-bearing, not isolated endurance.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:154-156 showed accompaniment operating through killing and calibrated subtraction, confirming relational load-bearing rather than escape from pressure.

## 2:154 — وَلَا تَقُولُوا۟ لِمَن يُقْتَلُ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ أَمْوَٰتٌۢ ۚ بَلْ أَحْيَآءٌۭ وَلَٰكِن لَّا تَشْعُرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Visible cessation does not exhaust life sustained by relation to the way.** `ق ت ل B001` supplies killing, `س ب ل B001` an extended traversed way, `م و ت B012` still lifeless inactivity, `ح ي ي B013` life as benefit or saving good, and `ش ع ر B005` subtle awareness. Those killed are located “in the way,” then the dead/alive classification is reversed while ordinary awareness is denied; 2:153 supplied divine accompaniment and 2:155 counts loss of lives among partial tests. The packet supplies path relation and perceptual limit; I infer that continued life is not accessible to surface classification.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:155 confirmed the perceptual limit by including lost selves within only a partial test; surface cessation does not exhaust the wider relation or its outcome.

## 2:155 — وَلَنَبْلُوَنَّكُم بِشَىْءٍۢ مِّنَ ٱلْخَوْفِ وَٱلْجُوعِ وَنَقْصٍۢ مِّنَ ٱلْأَمْوَٰلِ وَٱلْأَنفُسِ وَٱلثَّمَرَٰتِ ۗ وَبَشِّرِ ٱلصَّٰبِرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **The test is calibrated through partial subtraction rather than total annihilation.** `ب ل و B002` supplies testing that discloses state, `ش ي ء B001` a knowable portion, `ج و ع B004` hunger as emptiness, `ن ق ص B001` loss of a part, `م و ل B001` held wealth, `ن ف س B011` living selves, `ث م ر B003` benefit-fruit, and `ص ب ر B001` restraint. “Something of” and “decrease of” govern the list; 2:154 limits surface death classification, and 2:156 supplies a return response. I infer measured pressure designed to disclose orientation. Good news attaches to maintained restraint within subtraction.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:156 supplied the test's desired disclosure, as affliction elicits a source-and-return statement; 2:157 then places mercy and guidance upon that response.

## 2:156 — ٱلَّذِينَ إِذَآ أَصَٰبَتْهُم مُّصِيبَةٌۭ قَالُوٓا۟ إِنَّا لِلَّهِ وَإِنَّآ إِلَيْهِ رَٰجِعُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The impact re-aims identity toward source and destination.** Repeated `ص و ب B004` supplies an affliction that hits, while neighboring `ص و ب B002/B003` supplies correctness settled against error and hitting a target; `ر ج ع B001` is return. When the event strikes, the response places the speakers as belonging to God and moving back to him; 2:155 supplied calibrated loss and 2:157 places guidance upon the responders. The packet supplies hit, speech, and return; I infer that the utterance converts impact into corrected trajectory rather than uncontrolled displacement.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:157 confirmed trajectory correction through reciprocal placement: what struck “upon” them is answered by nurturing response “upon” them.

## 2:157 — أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ عَلَيْهِمْ صَلَوَٰتٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّهِمْ وَرَحْمَةٌۭ ۖ وَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ هُمُ ٱلْمُهْتَدُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **A declared return draws nurturing response onto the struck person and stabilizes direction.** `ص ل و B002` supplies supplication, praise, and mercy, `ر ب ب B002` nurture toward completion, `ر ح م B001` tenderness, and `ه د ي B010/B002` composed bearing and intended course. The repeated “upon them” answers the affliction that came upon them in 2:156; mercy and guidance follow their belonging-return statement, while 2:158 moves into embodied ritual circulation. The packet supplies reciprocal placement; I infer that divine response restores composure after impact. Guidance reads as recovered gait, not information alone.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:158 embodied recovered gait as legitimate circulation between ritual signs, confirming guidance as composed movement rather than information alone.

## 2:158 — ۞ إِنَّ ٱلصَّفَا وَٱلْمَرْوَةَ مِن شَعَآئِرِ ٱللَّهِ ۖ فَمَنْ حَجَّ ٱلْبَيْتَ أَوِ ٱعْتَمَرَ فَلَا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْهِ أَن يَطَّوَّفَ بِهِمَا ۚ وَمَن تَطَوَّعَ خَيْرًۭا فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ شَاكِرٌ عَلِيمٌ

### Activated readings

1. **A suspected side-lean is legitimized as ritual circulation and voluntary capacity.** `ش ع ر B006` supplies a ritual marking sign, `ح ج ج B001` directed going, `ب ي ت B001` shelter, `ع م ر B004` intentional visit, `ج ن ح B001/B003` leaning to a side or offense, `ط و ف B001` circling, and `ط و ع B004/B005` working oneself into ability or voluntary extra good. The ayah removes *junah* from circling two emblems, then praises voluntary increase; 2:157 restored guided gait after trial. I infer that embodied movement once read as deviation is reintegrated into the route. Divine gratitude acknowledges surplus willing motion.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:159 introduced a sharp contrast: signs made publicly traversable can still be resealed, so legitimate circulation heightens the seriousness of concealed access.

## 2:159 — إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ يَكْتُمُونَ مَآ أَنزَلْنَا مِنَ ٱلْبَيِّنَٰتِ وَٱلْهُدَىٰ مِنۢ بَعْدِ مَا بَيَّنَّٰهُ لِلنَّاسِ فِى ٱلْكِتَٰبِ ۙ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ يَلْعَنُهُمُ ٱللَّهُ وَيَلْعَنُهُمُ ٱللَّٰعِنُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Concealment severs a public guidance network and returns as social expulsion.** `ك ت م B004` supplies a seam sealed against flow, `ن ز ل B002` descent, `ب ي ن B005` disclosure, `ه د ي B002` course, `ك ت ب B001` joining, and `ل ع ن B001` punitive distancing. What descended was clarified for people in a joined book, yet is resealed; 2:158 made signs publicly traversable, while 2:160 requires repair plus renewed disclosure. The packet supplies circulation, blockage, and reciprocal cursing; I infer that suppressing shared access expels the suppressor from the very network withheld.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:160 explicitly confirmed the network diagnosis by requiring the concealer to repair and disclose, thereby restoring exactly the circulation that was blocked.

## 2:160 — إِلَّا ٱلَّذِينَ تَابُوا۟ وَأَصْلَحُوا۟ وَبَيَّنُوا۟ فَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ أَتُوبُ عَلَيْهِمْ ۚ وَأَنَا ٱلتَّوَّابُ ٱلرَّحِيمُ

### Activated readings

1. **Return must become public repair and restored disclosure.** `ص ل ح B002/B003` supplies reconciliation and making fit, `ب ي ن B004/B005` becoming clear and disclosing meaning, and `ر ح م B001` mercy. The exception does not stop at inward turning: it adds repair and the same disclosure withheld in 2:159; the divine response then turns toward them. The packet supplies three ordered corrections; I infer that restored circulation is the evidence that return has reversed concealment's damage. Repentance reads as relational repair with a public output, not private regret alone.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:161-162 supplied the terminal alternative: without public repair, covering hardens into gathered separation with neither reduction nor respite.

## 2:161 — إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ وَمَاتُوا۟ وَهُمْ كُفَّارٌ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ عَلَيْهِمْ لَعْنَةُ ٱللَّهِ وَٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةِ وَٱلنَّاسِ أَجْمَعِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Terminal covering reverses into total public separation.** `ك ف ر B001` supplies covering, `م و ت B001` death, `ل ع ن B001` punitive distancing, `م ل ك B001` cohesion, `ن و س B001` human movement, and `ج م ع B001` gathering all parts. The agents die while still coverers, and distancing comes from God, angels, and people all together; 2:159-160 had offered public disclosure and repair before this closure, while 2:162 removes respite. The packet supplies withheld circulation followed by gathered expulsion; I infer that terminal concealment fixes exclusion from every layer of the knowledge community.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:162 confirmed terminality along both intensity and time, while 2:163 made the lost alternative stark by naming one merciful source immediately afterward.

## 2:162 — خَٰلِدِينَ فِيهَا ۖ لَا يُخَفَّفُ عَنْهُمُ ٱلْعَذَابُ وَلَا هُمْ يُنظَرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The fixed state has neither reduced intensity nor extended time.** `خ ل د B002` supplies clinging and staying close, `خ ف ف B003` reduction in amount, `ع ذ ب B005` punishment, and `ن ظ ر B002` granting respite. The two negations remove a quantitative release and a temporal release; 2:161 supplied terminal gathered distancing, while 2:163 immediately names one merciful deity. The packet supplies persistence along both axes; I infer that no alternative attachment remains to loosen the state. Permanence reads as relational clinging whose load is neither lightened nor deferred.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:163 supplied contrast rather than correction: exclusive merciful unity stands beside a clinging state whose load is neither lightened nor deferred.

## 2:163 — وَإِلَٰهُكُمْ إِلَٰهٌۭ وَٰحِدٌۭ ۖ لَّآ إِلَٰهَ إِلَّا هُوَ ٱلرَّحْمَٰنُ ٱلرَّحِيمُ

### Activated readings

1. **Unity gathers worship and generative mercy into one source.** `ء ل ه B001` supplies worship and the worshipped one, `و ح د B004` exclusive divine oneness, `ر ح م B001` tenderness, and the dormant `ر ح م B003` womb image. The negation/exception closes every rival channel; 2:164 immediately unfolds a life-generating water-and-earth system, while 2:165 shows rival love and later severed means. The packet supplies unity, mercy, and generated life; I infer that the one source holds creation in a mercy-like enclosure. Divine unity reads causally as coherence of dependence, not number alone.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:164 strongly confirmed generative unity by unfolding sky, earth, water, life, wind, and cloud as one tethered circulation system.

## 2:164 — إِنَّ فِى خَلْقِ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ وَٱخْتِلَٰفِ ٱلَّيْلِ وَٱلنَّهَارِ وَٱلْفُلْكِ ٱلَّتِى تَجْرِى فِى ٱلْبَحْرِ بِمَا يَنفَعُ ٱلنَّاسَ وَمَآ أَنزَلَ ٱللَّهُ مِنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ مِن مَّآءٍۢ فَأَحْيَا بِهِ ٱلْأَرْضَ بَعْدَ مَوْتِهَا وَبَثَّ فِيهَا مِن كُلِّ دَآبَّةٍۢ وَتَصْرِيفِ ٱلرِّيَٰحِ وَٱلسَّحَابِ ٱلْمُسَخَّرِ بَيْنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ لَءَايَٰتٍۢ لِّقَوْمٍۢ يَعْقِلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The signs form a coupled circulation system rather than a catalog of objects.** `خ ل ق B001` supplies measured proportion, `خ ل ف B006` alternation, `ف ل ك B001/B003` circular motion and ship, `ج ر ي B001` flow, `ن ز ل B002` descent, `ح ي ي B002` rain-grown life, `ب ث ث B001` spreading, `ص ر ف B002` turning through states, `ر و ح B003` wind, `س ح ب B002` drawn cloud, `س خ ر B001` commanded purpose, and `ع ق ل B002` tethering. The sequence links alternation, transport, benefit, rain, revival, dispersal, wind, and cloud between sky and earth. I infer causal arrows among the supplied links. Reason reads as tethering processes into one life-support mechanism.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:165-166 exposed the system reading's social consequence: rival supports are mistaken for independent sources, then their connecting means fail under load.

## 2:165 — وَمِنَ ٱلنَّاسِ مَن يَتَّخِذُ مِن دُونِ ٱللَّهِ أَندَادًۭا يُحِبُّونَهُمْ كَحُبِّ ٱللَّهِ ۖ وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوٓا۟ أَشَدُّ حُبًّۭا لِّلَّهِ ۗ وَلَوْ يَرَى ٱلَّذِينَ ظَلَمُوٓا۟ إِذْ يَرَوْنَ ٱلْعَذَابَ أَنَّ ٱلْقُوَّةَ لِلَّهِ جَمِيعًۭا وَأَنَّ ٱللَّهَ شَدِيدُ ٱلْعَذَابِ

### Activated readings

1. **Love attaches to an imagined support distribution until force is disclosed as gathered.** `ن د د B002` supplies rival counterpart, `ح ب ب B002` clinging love, `ش د د B001/B002` fastening and intensity, `ق و ي B001` rope-like assembled strength, and `ج م ع B001` total gathering. Rivals receive God-like attachment, but the future sight gathers all strength to God; 2:163-164 already gave one source and one integrated system, while 2:166 cuts the followers' connecting ropes. The packet supplies attachment, force, and severance; I infer that rival love mistakes delegated links for independent supports. Stronger faithful love attaches to the actual source of cohesion.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:166 directly confirmed the delegated-link inference when leaders release their claims and the ropes connecting followers are cut.

## 2:166 — إِذْ تَبَرَّأَ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱتُّبِعُوا۟ مِنَ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱتَّبَعُوا۟ وَرَأَوُا۟ ٱلْعَذَابَ وَتَقَطَّعَتْ بِهِمُ ٱلْأَسْبَابُ

### Activated readings

1. **The follower hierarchy fails when its mediating ropes are load-tested.** `ب ر ء B004` supplies release of a claim and parting, repeated `ت ب ع B003` stepwise following, `ر ء ي B001` sight, `ق ط ع B007` severance of connection, and `س ب ب B003` a rope or means. Those followed clear themselves of followers as punishment becomes visible, and the means connecting them are cut; 2:165 supplied rival attachment, while 2:167 asks for a return to reverse the disavowal. The packet supplies hierarchy, claim-release, and broken ropes; I infer that the network lacked reciprocal responsibility. Mediation collapses under consequence.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:167 showed the failed hierarchy becoming irreversible work-history, and 2:168 converted that failure into a warning against incorporating another stepwise path.

## 2:167 — وَقَالَ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱتَّبَعُوا۟ لَوْ أَنَّ لَنَا كَرَّةًۭ فَنَتَبَرَّأَ مِنْهُمْ كَمَا تَبَرَّءُوا۟ مِنَّا ۗ كَذَٰلِكَ يُرِيهِمُ ٱللَّهُ أَعْمَٰلَهُمْ حَسَرَٰتٍ عَلَيْهِمْ ۖ وَمَا هُم بِخَٰرِجِينَ مِنَ ٱلنَّارِ

### Activated readings

1. **The wished return exposes disavowal as too late to alter an already visible work-history.** `ت ب ع B003` is tracking, `ك ر ر B001` returning for another turn, `ب ر ء B004` releasing claims, `ر ء ي B012` making seen, `ع م ل B001` intentional work, `ح س ر B004` grief over what was missed, and `خ ر ج B001` exit. Followers want one repetition to mirror their leaders' repudiation, but their acts are shown as griefs and no exit follows; 2:168 warns against stepwise following. The packet supplies impossible replay and fixed output; I infer that reciprocal blame cannot rewrite the path already walked.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:168-169 confirmed that the path already walked cannot be rewritten by reciprocal blame; future protection begins by discriminating intake and followed directives now.

## 2:168 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلنَّاسُ كُلُوا۟ مِمَّا فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ حَلَٰلًۭا طَيِّبًۭا وَلَا تَتَّبِعُوا۟ خُطُوَٰتِ ٱلشَّيْطَٰنِ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ لَكُمْ عَدُوٌّۭ مُّبِينٌ

### Activated readings

1. **Bodily intake and moral walking are paired as two forms of incorporation.** `ء ك ل B001` supplies eating, `ح ل ل B001/B003` untying a knot and removal of prohibition, `ط ي ب B002` untainted taking, `ت ب ع B003` stepwise tracking, `خ ط و B002` followed traces, `ش ط ن B003` crooked opposition, and `ع د و B001` boundary overstep. After failed follower ropes in 2:166-167, the command permits pure earth-food but forbids hostile steps; 2:169 shows where those steps lead. I infer that repeated intake forms body while repeated following forms conduct. Both require source discrimination.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:169 specified the moral incorporation sequence as harm, excess, and false authorization; 2:170 then showed inherited tracks reproducing it without tethered judgment.

## 2:169 — إِنَّمَا يَأْمُرُكُم بِٱلسُّوٓءِ وَٱلْفَحْشَآءِ وَأَن تَقُولُوا۟ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The hostile path escalates from harm, through boundary excess, to false authorization.** `ء م ر B002` supplies directive, `س و ء B002` harm, `ف ح ش B002/B003` exceeding the proper bound in conduct or speech, `ق و ل B001` utterance, and `ع ل م B001` clear knowledge. The ordered objects follow the prohibited steps of 2:168 and precede inherited following without reason in 2:170. The packet supplies behavioral and epistemic escalation; I infer that unsupported God-speech retrospectively legitimizes the earlier harmful steps. Fabricated authority is the path's culmination, not a separate error.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:170-171 confirmed the escalation: unsupported divine speech becomes ancestral proof, then meaningful address is compressed into call and noise without semantic uptake.

## 2:170 — وَإِذَا قِيلَ لَهُمُ ٱتَّبِعُوا۟ مَآ أَنزَلَ ٱللَّهُ قَالُوا۟ بَلْ نَتَّبِعُ مَآ أَلْفَيْنَا عَلَيْهِ ءَابَآءَنَآ ۗ أَوَلَوْ كَانَ ءَابَآؤُهُمْ لَا يَعْقِلُونَ شَيْـًۭٔا وَلَا يَهْتَدُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Mere foundness substitutes for a tether between inheritance and guidance.** Repeated `ت ب ع B003` supplies stepwise tracking, `ن ز ل B002` descent, `ء ب و B001` parental nurture, `ع ق ل B002` tethering, `ش ي ء B001` a knowable thing, and `ه د ي B002` intended course. The speakers reject descended guidance for what they found ancestors upon, even under the stated possibility that those ancestors neither tethered knowledge nor found a course; 2:169 supplied unsupported authority, and 2:171 reduces reception to sound. I infer that repetition alone is being used as proof. Ancestry becomes an unexamined track rather than transmitted nurture.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:171 confirmed that foundness is not guidance, because repeated inherited signal can persist after the tether between content and response has broken.

## 2:171 — وَمَثَلُ ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ كَمَثَلِ ٱلَّذِى يَنْعِقُ بِمَا لَا يَسْمَعُ إِلَّا دُعَآءًۭ وَنِدَآءًۭ ۚ صُمٌّۢ بُكْمٌ عُمْىٌۭ فَهُمْ لَا يَعْقِلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Meaningful address is compressed into undifferentiated acoustic signal.** `ن ع ق B001` supplies a shepherd's driving call, `س م ع B011` hearing without reaching, `د ع و B001` a call drawing near, `ن د و B002` raised voice, `ص م م B002` a stopped opening, `ب ك م B002` inability to answer, `ع م ي B004` obscuring, and `ع ق ل B002` tethering. The hearer retains only call and cry; the sensory closures then prevent return linkage. The packet supplies sound without semantic arrival; I infer that ancestral repetition in 2:170 has trained response to signal rather than content. Reason fails as a broken communication tether.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:172 supplied the positive communication circuit: pure provision is received and returned as visible gratitude rather than retained as undifferentiated signal.

## 2:172 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ كُلُوا۟ مِن طَيِّبَٰتِ مَا رَزَقْنَٰكُمْ وَٱشْكُرُوا۟ لِلَّهِ إِن كُنتُمْ إِيَّاهُ تَعْبُدُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Pure provision should pass through the body and return as visible gratitude.** `ء ك ل B001` supplies eating, `ط ي ب B002` untainted taking, `ر ز ق B001` allotted gift, `ش ك ر B002` showing sustenance's effect, and `ع ب د B003` worshipful obedience. The command narrows the all-human food address of 2:168 to believers and ties intake to thanks; 2:173 defines the food boundary, while 2:174 depicts concealed revelation consumed as fire. The packet supplies two ingestion outputs; I infer that gratitude keeps source visible. Worship is tested by what nourishment becomes after reception.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:173-174 confirmed that nourishment's output tests reception: bounded food becomes gratitude, while sealed revelation becomes destructive inward consumption.

## 2:173 — إِنَّمَا حَرَّمَ عَلَيْكُمُ ٱلْمَيْتَةَ وَٱلدَّمَ وَلَحْمَ ٱلْخِنزِيرِ وَمَآ أُهِلَّ بِهِۦ لِغَيْرِ ٱللَّهِ ۖ فَمَنِ ٱضْطُرَّ غَيْرَ بَاغٍۢ وَلَا عَادٍۢ فَلَآ إِثْمَ عَلَيْهِ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ غَفُورٌۭ رَّحِيمٌ

### Activated readings

1. **The food boundary opens under necessity but carries guardrails against expansion.** `ح ر م B003` supplies protected bounds, `ه ل ل B004` raised proclamation, `ض ر ر B003` compulsion under necessity, `ب غ ي B003` oppressive overstepping, `ع د و B001` crossing a bound, `ء ث م B001` lagging from good, `غ ف ر B001` protective covering, and `ر ح م B001` mercy. Prohibited items are followed by an exception conditioned on neither seeking nor overstepping; 2:172 linked food to worship and 2:174 monetizes concealment. The packet supplies rule, pressure valve, and limits; I infer adaptive protection rather than brittle prohibition.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:174 sharpened the mercy of the necessity exception by contrast: adaptive bodily intake protects life, whereas monetized concealment reroutes public nourishment into fire.

## 2:174 — إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ يَكْتُمُونَ مَآ أَنزَلَ ٱللَّهُ مِنَ ٱلْكِتَٰبِ وَيَشْتَرُونَ بِهِۦ ثَمَنًۭا قَلِيلًا ۙ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ مَا يَأْكُلُونَ فِى بُطُونِهِمْ إِلَّا ٱلنَّارَ وَلَا يُكَلِّمُهُمُ ٱللَّهُ يَوْمَ ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ وَلَا يُزَكِّيهِمْ وَلَهُمْ عَذَابٌ أَلِيمٌ

### Activated readings

1. **Privatized revelation becomes toxic inward consumption and severs dialogue.** `ك ت م B004` supplies sealed seams, `ن ز ل B002` descent, `ك ت ب B001` joining, `ش ر ي B001` exchange, `ء ك ل B001` eating, `ب ط ن B002` hidden inward reality, `ن و ر B002` burning fire, `ك ل م B001` exchanged speech, and `ز ك و B001` growth. Concealment and sale are redescribed as eating fire into bellies, followed by no divine speech and no purification; 2:172-173 supplied clean intake. I infer that blocking public nourishment reroutes its value inward as destructive gain. The transaction consumes the seller's capacity for dialogue and growth.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:175 named the toxic consumption as a double exchange, and 2:176 showed its communal result as schism produced inside a joining book.

## 2:175 — أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱشْتَرَوُا۟ ٱلضَّلَٰلَةَ بِٱلْهُدَىٰ وَٱلْعَذَابَ بِٱلْمَغْفِرَةِ ۚ فَمَآ أَصْبَرَهُمْ عَلَى ٱلنَّارِ

### Activated readings

1. **The double exchange inverts restraint into compelled holding on fire.** `ش ر ي B001` supplies exchange, `ض ل ل B001` loss of aim, `ه د ي B002` intended course, `ع ذ ب B005` punishment, `غ ف ر B002` shielding forgiveness, `ص ب ر B002` compelled holding, and `ن و ر B002` burning fire. The buyers substitute disorientation for course and punishment for cover; 2:174 made the price edible fire, and 2:176 locates disagreement inside the book. The packet supplies paired trades and astonishing patience; I infer that repeated choice fixes them to what harms. *Asbarahum* reads as a grimly manufactured endurance, not virtue.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:176 confirmed manufactured endurance as relational fixation: disagreement cuts within the connector and pushes its buyers into distant schism.

## 2:176 — ذَٰلِكَ بِأَنَّ ٱللَّهَ نَزَّلَ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ بِٱلْحَقِّ ۗ وَإِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱخْتَلَفُوا۟ فِى ٱلْكِتَٰبِ لَفِى شِقَاقٍۭ بَعِيدٍۢ

### Activated readings

1. **Splitting inside a joining medium generates maximal relational distance.** `ن ز ل B002` supplies descent, `ك ت ب B001` joining, `ح ق ق B010` tight firmness, `خ ل ف B004` taking different ways, `ش ق ق B004` group splitting, and `ب ع د B001` remoteness. The book descends joined with truth, yet disagreement occurs “in” it and yields far schism; 2:174-175 showed concealment and exchange, while 2:177 rebuilds righteousness across domains. The packet supplies container and split; I infer that cutting within an intended connector pushes parties farther apart than external difference would. The distance is produced, not given.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:177 supplied the repair architecture, rebuilding joined truth across belief, redistribution, worship, covenant, and endurance rather than one contested textual coordinate.

## 2:177 — ۞ لَّيْسَ ٱلْبِرَّ أَن تُوَلُّوا۟ وُجُوهَكُمْ قِبَلَ ٱلْمَشْرِقِ وَٱلْمَغْرِبِ وَلَٰكِنَّ ٱلْبِرَّ مَنْ ءَامَنَ بِٱللَّهِ وَٱلْيَوْمِ ٱلْءَاخِرِ وَٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةِ وَٱلْكِتَٰبِ وَٱلنَّبِيِّۦنَ وَءَاتَى ٱلْمَالَ عَلَىٰ حُبِّهِۦ ذَوِى ٱلْقُرْبَىٰ وَٱلْيَتَٰمَىٰ وَٱلْمَسَٰكِينَ وَٱبْنَ ٱلسَّبِيلِ وَٱلسَّآئِلِينَ وَفِى ٱلرِّقَابِ وَأَقَامَ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ وَءَاتَى ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ وَٱلْمُوفُونَ بِعَهْدِهِمْ إِذَا عَٰهَدُوا۟ ۖ وَٱلصَّٰبِرِينَ فِى ٱلْبَأْسَآءِ وَٱلضَّرَّآءِ وَحِينَ ٱلْبَأْسِ ۗ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ ٱلَّذِينَ صَدَقُوا۟ ۖ وَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ هُمُ ٱلْمُتَّقُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Righteousness is cross-domain integrity that makes conviction hold under transfer and pressure.** `ب ر ر B001/B002` supplies truth that makes word or deed hold and expansive obedience, `و ج ه B002` direction, `ح ب ب B002` attachment, `ي ت م B001` lost protection, `ر ق ب B004` a person in bondage, `و ف ي B001` full completion, `ع ه د B001` maintained charge, `ص ب ر B001` restraint, `ص د ق B004` fulfillment, and `و ق ي B001` protection. The ayah displaces east-west facing with belief, costly redistribution, ritual, covenant, and endurance. I infer that coherence across these stress points verifies truth. Direction is one coordinate; *birr* is the whole load-bearing structure.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:178-180 tested cross-domain integrity under killing and death, confirming that the structure must hold through equivalent response, pardon, payment, and bequest.

## 2:178 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ كُتِبَ عَلَيْكُمُ ٱلْقِصَاصُ فِى ٱلْقَتْلَى ۖ ٱلْحُرُّ بِٱلْحُرِّ وَٱلْعَبْدُ بِٱلْعَبْدِ وَٱلْأُنثَىٰ بِٱلْأُنثَىٰ ۚ فَمَنْ عُفِىَ لَهُۥ مِنْ أَخِيهِ شَىْءٌۭ فَٱتِّبَاعٌۢ بِٱلْمَعْرُوفِ وَأَدَآءٌ إِلَيْهِ بِإِحْسَٰنٍۢ ۗ ذَٰلِكَ تَخْفِيفٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّكُمْ وَرَحْمَةٌۭ ۗ فَمَنِ ٱعْتَدَىٰ بَعْدَ ذَٰلِكَ فَلَهُۥ عَذَابٌ أَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Retaliation is bounded trace-following with a brotherhood off-ramp.** `ك ت ب B003` supplies binding prescription, `ق ص ص B003` equivalent retaliation, `ق ت ل B001` killing, `ع ف و B001` wiping guilt, `ء خ و B001/B002` brotherhood and tether, `ت ب ع B003` tracked following, `ع ر ف B005` recognized good, `ء د ي B002` fulfilling what is due, `ح س ن B002` skillful good, and `خ ف ف B001` lightening. Matching categories constrain equivalence; pardon then changes the path to recognized pursuit and beautiful payment. The packet supplies limit and alternative; I infer that mercy interrupts vengeance without erasing due process.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:179 explicitly located life in bounded equivalence, confirming that pardon is an off-ramp within due process rather than a denial of the victim's claim.

## 2:179 — وَلَكُمْ فِى ٱلْقِصَاصِ حَيَوٰةٌۭ يَٰٓأُو۟لِى ٱلْأَلْبَٰبِ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَتَّقُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Equivalent trace-following protects life by containing recursive violence.** `ق ص ص B001/B003` supplies following a trace and equivalent retaliation, `ح ي ي B013` life as saving good, `ل ب ب B003/B004` pure core and purified intellect, and `و ق ي B001` a protective barrier. The claim follows 2:178's matched categories, pardon, and payment, then 2:180 redirects death into ordered bequest. The packet supplies bounded response and preserved continuity; I infer that predictable equivalence prevents retaliation from multiplying beyond its trace. The pure intellect sees life not in killing itself but in the limit placed around recurrence.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:180 extended preserved continuity beyond physical survival through bequest, and 2:181 protected that forward joining from later alteration.

## 2:180 — كُتِبَ عَلَيْكُمْ إِذَا حَضَرَ أَحَدَكُمُ ٱلْمَوْتُ إِن تَرَكَ خَيْرًا ٱلْوَصِيَّةُ لِلْوَٰلِدَيْنِ وَٱلْأَقْرَبِينَ بِٱلْمَعْرُوفِ ۖ حَقًّا عَلَى ٱلْمُتَّقِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Death turns held benefit into a relationally directed charge rather than ownerless remainder.** `ك ت ب B003` is binding prescription, `ح ض ر B009` decisive attendance by death, `ت ر ك B006` what the dead leave, `خ ي ر B005` beneficence, `و ص ي B001/B002` joining and an attached charge, `و ل د B002` parents, `ق ر ب B003` kinship, `ع ر ف B005` recognized good, and `ح ق ق B002` due obligation. After life-preserving retaliation (2:178-179), property at death is joined forward to relations; 2:181 guards the transfer from alteration. I infer that bequest preserves social continuity across the owner's endpoint.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:181 confirmed custody as a new accountable act, while 2:182 supplied the narrow surprise that faithful transmission may require repair when the original charge visibly leans.

## 2:181 — فَمَنۢ بَدَّلَهُۥ بَعْدَمَا سَمِعَهُۥ فَإِنَّمَآ إِثْمُهُۥ عَلَى ٱلَّذِينَ يُبَدِّلُونَهُۥٓ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ سَمِيعٌ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **A heard bequest remains joined to its source, so later alteration transfers liability to the alterer.** The repeated `ب د ل B001/B002` frames substitution as transformation of the received charge, while `س م ع B003` makes hearing an act of understanding and compliance and `ء ث م B004` loads the resulting fault onto those who change it. The after-hearing sequence links back to the joined charge of 2:180 and forward to 2:182's narrow repair exception. I infer that transmission is not neutral copying: once the charge is understood, changing it creates a new accountable act rather than revising the deceased person's act.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:182 supplied the crucial correction: not every intervention is illicit substitution, because a detectable lean can require reconciliatory repair that preserves the charge's purpose.

## 2:182 — فَمَنْ خَافَ مِن مُّوصٍۢ جَنَفًا أَوْ إِثْمًۭ فَأَصْلَحَ بَيْنَهُمْ فَلَآ إِثْمَ عَلَيْهِ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ غَفُورٌۭ رَّحِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Repair is distinguished from alteration by an observable lean away from upright relation.** `و ص ي B001/B002` keeps the bequest as an attached charge, `ج ن ف B001` marks a sideways lean in judgment or intent, `ص ل ح B002` assigns reconciliation the role of restoring relation, `ب ي ن B003` locates that restoration in the tie among the parties, and `ء ث م B001/B004` distinguishes obstructive guilt from the repair itself. The exception follows 2:181's prohibition and precedes fasting's disciplined restraint in 2:183. I infer that faithful custody can require intervention when literal preservation would perpetuate a detectable relational distortion.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:183-187 related faithful repair to disciplined boundaries, confirming that intervention must be structured and purpose-preserving rather than discretionary rewriting.

## 2:183 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ كُتِبَ عَلَيْكُمُ ٱلصِّيَامُ كَمَا كُتِبَ عَلَى ٱلَّذِينَ مِن قَبْلِكُمْ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَتَّقُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Fasting is a prescribed withholding that trains deliberate self-placement behind a barrier.** `ك ت ب B003` makes the practice binding, `ص و م B001` supplies fasting as withholding, and `و ق ي B001/B002` gives the intended result as both protection and cautious self-positioning. The comparison with those before makes this prescription a transmitted discipline, while 2:184-187 specify its counted, embodied limits. I infer that restraint is not the mere absence of eating but repeated practice in locating oneself relative to a boundary.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:184-185 made the barrier adaptive rather than brittle through replacement counts, feeding, ease, and common completion; 2:187 then supplied exact temporal edges.

## 2:184 — أَيَّامًۭا مَّعْدُودَٰتٍۢ ۚ فَمَن كَانَ مِنكُم مَّرِيضًا أَوْ عَلَىٰ سَفَرٍۢ فَعِدَّةٌۭ مِّنْ أَيَّامٍ أُخَرَ ۚ وَعَلَى ٱلَّذِينَ يُطِيقُونَهُۥ فِدْيَةٌۭ طَعَامُ مِسْكِينٍۢ ۖ فَمَن تَطَوَّعَ خَيْرًۭا فَهُوَ خَيْرٌۭ لَّهُۥ ۚ وَأَن تَصُومُوا۟ خَيْرٌۭ لَّكُمْ ۖ إِن كُنتُمْ تَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The counted discipline preserves its purpose by allowing measured substitutions for disrupted capacity.** `ع د د B003/B005` makes the days a prescribed count, `س ف ر B003` and bodily illness interrupt ordinary placement, `ط و ق B002` foregrounds capacity or imposed burden, `ف د ي B001` supplies a substitute, and `ط ع م B001/B004` turns that substitute into food provision for `س ك ن B006`, poverty. `ط و ع B005` then opens a voluntary surplus without dissolving `ص و م B001` as the preferred withholding. I infer from the replacement count and feeding option that equivalence protects disciplined participation without pretending that every body occupies the same conditions.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:185 explicitly governed substitution by ease and completion, strongly confirming that differentiated capacity can preserve one shared discipline.

## 2:185 — شَهْرُ رَمَضَانَ ٱلَّذِىٓ أُنزِلَ فِيهِ ٱلْقُرْءَانُ هُدًۭى لِّلنَّاسِ وَبَيِّنَٰتٍۢ مِّنَ ٱلْهُدَىٰ وَٱلْفُرْقَانِ ۚ فَمَن شَهِدَ مِنكُمُ ٱلشَّهْرَ فَلْيَصُمْهُ ۖ وَمَن كَانَ مَرِيضًا أَوْ عَلَىٰ سَفَرٍۢ فَعِدَّةٌۭ مِّنْ أَيَّامٍ أُخَرَ ۗ يُرِيدُ ٱللَّهُ بِكُمُ ٱلْيُسْرَ وَلَا يُرِيدُ بِكُمُ ٱلْعُسْرَ وَلِتُكْمِلُوا۟ ٱلْعِدَّةَ وَلِتُكَبِّرُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ عَلَىٰ مَا هَدَىٰكُمْ وَلَعَلَّكُمْ تَشْكُرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The public month coordinates bodily withholding with a recited course that makes distinctions usable.** `ش ه ر B001/B002` combines calendrical recognition with public exposure, `ق ر ء B001/B011` presents the recitation as gathered speech and directed course, `ه د ي B002` assigns it an intended path, and `ف ر ق B001` makes distinction part of that guidance. The repeated replacement count from 2:184 is now governed by `ي س ر B001`, ease and opening, rather than `ع س ر B001`, difficulty, while `ك م ل B001` and `ش ك ر B002` make completion display the guidance's sustaining effect. I infer that the month is a shared calibration period: differentiated accommodations serve, rather than weaken, common completion.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:186-187 made the public calibration unexpectedly relational and embodied: direct response stands inside detailed permissions, intimacy, and time-bounds.

## 2:186 — وَإِذَا سَأَلَكَ عِبَادِى عَنِّى فَإِنِّى قَرِيبٌ ۖ أُجِيبُ دَعْوَةَ ٱلدَّاعِ إِذَا دَعَانِ ۖ فَلْيَسْتَجِيبُوا۟ لِى وَلْيُؤْمِنُوا۟ بِى لَعَلَّهُمْ يَرْشُدُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Nearness turns invocation into a reciprocal opening rather than a one-way report through an intermediary.** `ق ر ب B001` supplies nearness, `د ع و B001` repeatedly brings the caller and called into proximity, and `ج و ب B003/B004` gives answering both response and the opening of a gap; `ر ش د B001/B002` names the hoped-for result as straight guidance and sound judgment. Placed inside the fasting rules of 2:183-187, this exchange interrupts regulation with direct relation. I infer that responsive discipline depends on an accessible addressee: the caller's answering response is itself part of becoming rightly directed.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:187 confirmed that reciprocal divine response does not bypass regulation; it enables agents to inhabit clarified bodily limits without concealment.

## 2:187 — أُحِلَّ لَكُمْ لَيْلَةَ ٱلصِّيَامِ ٱلرَّفَثُ إِلَىٰ نِسَآئِكُمْ ۚ هُنَّ لِبَاسٌۭ لَّكُمْ وَأَنتُمْ لِبَاسٌۭ لَّهُنَّ ۗ عَلِمَ ٱللَّهُ أَنَّكُمْ كُنتُمْ تَخْتَانُونَ أَنفُسَكُمْ فَتَابَ عَلَيْكُمْ وَعَفَا عَنكُمْ ۖ فَٱلْـَٰٔنَ بَٰشِرُوهُنَّ وَٱبْتَغُوا۟ مَا كَتَبَ ٱللَّهُ لَكُمْ ۚ وَكُلُوا۟ وَٱشْرَبُوا۟ حَتَّىٰ يَتَبَيَّنَ لَكُمُ ٱلْخَيْطُ ٱلْأَبْيَضُ مِنَ ٱلْخَيْطِ ٱلْأَسْوَدِ مِنَ ٱلْفَجْرِ ۖ ثُمَّ أَتِمُّوا۟ ٱلصِّيَامَ إِلَى ٱلْلَيْلِ ۚ وَلَا تُبَٰشِرُوهُنَّ وَأَنتُمْ عَٰكِفُونَ فِى ٱلْمَسَٰجِدِ ۗ تِلْكَ حُدُودُ ٱللَّهِ فَلَا تَقْرَبُوهَا ۗ كَذَٰلِكَ يُبَيِّنُ ٱللَّهُ ءَايَٰتِهِۦ لِلنَّاسِ لَعَلَّهُمْ يَتَّقُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The fast is made practicable through bright temporal edges and reciprocal bodily covering, not through indefinite deprivation.** `ح ل ل B003` removes prohibition at night, `ل ب س B001/B004` makes spouses mutual covering and intimacy, `ب ش ر B003` names direct bodily contact, and `ع ف و B001` wipes the prior breach marked by `خ و ن B001`. The white and black `خ ي ط B001` become clear through `ب ي ن B004` at `ف ج ر B002`, after which `ت م م B001` carries `ص و م B001` to night; `ح د د B001/B002` assigns these points the role of protective bounds. I infer that explicit permission and explicit endpoints stabilize restraint by preventing the boundary from becoming either secret self-betrayal or limitless severity.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:188-189 extended the boundary logic from body and time to wealth and access, confirming that clear limits must also govern social procedure.

## 2:188 — وَلَا تَأْكُلُوٓا۟ أَمْوَٰلَكُم بَيْنَكُم بِٱلْبَٰطِلِ وَتُدْلُوا۟ بِهَآ إِلَى ٱلْحُكَّامِ لِتَأْكُلُوا۟ فَرِيقًۭا مِّنْ أَمْوَٰلِ ٱلنَّاسِ بِٱلْإِثْمِ وَأَنتُمْ تَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Illicit wealth transfer is pictured as social consumption routed through adjudicative access.** `ء ك ل B004` twice makes appropriation the consuming of wealth, `م و ل B001` supplies the held resources, `ب ي ن B003` locates the damage within communal ties, and `ب ط ل B001` marks the route as void. `د ل و B004/B009` then portrays lowering a means toward `ح ك م B002`, adjudicators, including deceptive access, to consume a `ف ر ق B005`, partition, under `ء ث م B004`, loaded fault. Coming after precise bodily limits in 2:187 and before proper entrances in 2:189, I infer that legal procedure can become a false doorway when it is used to convert known voidness into an enforceable share.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:189 directly reinforced the false-doorway inference by commanding entry through fitting doors; formal access is valid only when its route matches recognized good.

## 2:189 — ۞ يَسْـَٔلُونَكَ عَنِ ٱلْأَهِلَّةِ ۖ قُلْ هِىَ مَوَٰقِيتُ لِلنَّاسِ وَٱلْحَجِّ ۗ وَلَيْسَ ٱلْبِرُّ بِأَن تَأْتُوا۟ ٱلْبُيُوتَ مِن ظُهُورِهَا وَلَٰكِنَّ ٱلْبِرَّ مَنِ ٱتَّقَىٰ ۗ وَأْتُوا۟ ٱلْبُيُوتَ مِنْ أَبْوَٰبِهَا ۚ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ لَعَلَّكُمْ تُفْلِحُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Visible cycles orient shared action, but righteousness still requires using the fitting entrance.** `ه ل ل B001` gives the crescents their observable form, `و ق ت B001/B002` makes them known bounds for people and `ح ج ج B001`, directed pilgrimage, while `ب ر ر B001/B002` relocates goodness from an eccentric approach to expansive right conduct. `ب ي ت B001` is the shelter, `ظ ه ر B012` casts the rear as what is put behind, and `ب و ب B001` supplies the proper entrance; `و ق ي B002` and `ف ل ح B005` connect that orientation to cautious placement and lasting good. I infer that signs do not authorize ritual ingenuity by themselves: their role is to coordinate direct, intelligible participation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:190-194 generalized fitting entry into conflict thresholds, where initiation, sanctuary, cessation, and equivalence determine whether action remains legitimate.

## 2:190 — وَقَٰتِلُوا۟ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ ٱلَّذِينَ يُقَٰتِلُونَكُمْ وَلَا تَعْتَدُوٓا۟ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ لَا يُحِبُّ ٱلْمُعْتَدِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Combat is grammatically reciprocal and morally bounded by the prohibition on crossing beyond its initiating relation.** `ق ت ل B011` assigns the repeated form mutual combat, `س ب ل B001` locates it on an extended way rather than making violence an end, and `ع د و B001` marks transgression as crossing the limit; `ح ب ب B002` denies clinging approval to those who do so. The relative clause restricts the object to those fighting, and 2:192-194 repeatedly require cessation and equivalent response. I infer that the command is conditioned resistance whose legitimacy collapses when reciprocity becomes excess.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:191-194 repeatedly confirmed conditioned resistance through sanctuary limits, immediate stopping, a stated terminus, and counterpart response.

## 2:191 — وَٱقْتُلُوهُمْ حَيْثُ ثَقِفْتُمُوهُمْ وَأَخْرِجُوهُم مِّنْ حَيْثُ أَخْرَجُوكُمْ ۚ وَٱلْفِتْنَةُ أَشَدُّ مِنَ ٱلْقَتْلِ ۚ وَلَا تُقَٰتِلُوهُمْ عِندَ ٱلْمَسْجِدِ ٱلْحَرَامِ حَتَّىٰ يُقَٰتِلُوكُمْ فِيهِ ۖ فَإِن قَٰتَلُوكُمْ فَٱقْتُلُوهُمْ ۗ كَذَٰلِكَ جَزَآءُ ٱلْكَٰفِرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **The severe response tracks prior expulsion and coercive disruption, while the sanctuary preserves an initiating threshold.** `ق ت ل B001/B011` distinguishes killing from mutual combat, `ث ق ف B003` marks getting hold of the opposing force, and `ف ت ن B003/B006` supplies diversion from the right and conflict as the condition judged more intense through `ش د د B002`. At `ح ر م B003`, protected bounds suspend combat until the opponents initiate it there, and `ج ز ي B001` frames the result as matching recompense. Read with 2:190's non-transgression and 2:192's immediate cessation, I infer that the spatially broad language remains response-indexed rather than becoming an unconditioned license.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:192 was the decisive constraint: present cessation immediately changes the relation; 2:193-194 then restated endpoint and equivalence as additional ceilings.

## 2:192 — فَإِنِ ٱنتَهَوْا۟ فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ غَفُورٌۭ رَّحِيمٌ

### Activated readings

1. **Cessation is a decisive endpoint that changes the governing relation from combat to protective forgiveness.** `ن ه ي B002` gives stopping the force of reaching an end, and `غ ف ر B001/B002` supplies both protective covering and forgiveness. Its compact conditional interrupts the combat sequence before 2:193 restates the aim and again bars aggression after cessation. I infer that the opponent's present stopping, not an endless memory of prior hostility, controls whether force may continue.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:193 repeated cessation and barred renewed aggression, confirming that forgiveness follows a real endpoint rather than indefinite memory of hostility.

## 2:193 — وَقَٰتِلُوهُمْ حَتَّىٰ لَا تَكُونَ فِتْنَةٌۭ وَيَكُونَ ٱلدِّينُ لِلَّهِ ۖ فَإِنِ ٱنتَهَوْا۟ فَلَا عُدْوَٰنَ إِلَّا عَلَى ٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **The stated terminus of combat is the removal of coercive disruption, not the indefinite subjection of a population.** `ق ت ل B011` retains reciprocal combat, `ف ت ن B003/B006` marks diversion and conflict, and `د ي ن B001/B006` places obedience and ordered authority with God. The repeated `ن ه ي B002` makes cessation an endpoint, after which `ع د و B001` is prohibited except against active wrongdoers. I infer from the paired until-clause and stop-clause that force has a self-canceling objective: once obstruction ends, continuing would reproduce the very transgression opposed in 2:190.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:194 translated the self-canceling aim into measured counterpart response, and 2:195 redirected communal energy from force toward sustaining expenditure.

## 2:194 — ٱلشَّهْرُ ٱلْحَرَامُ بِٱلشَّهْرِ ٱلْحَرَامِ وَٱلْحُرُمَٰتُ قِصَاصٌۭ ۚ فَمَنِ ٱعْتَدَىٰ عَلَيْكُمْ فَٱعْتَدُوا۟ عَلَيْهِ بِمِثْلِ مَا ٱعْتَدَىٰ عَلَيْكُمْ ۚ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ مَعَ ٱلْمُتَّقِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Sacred-time violation permits only a counterpart response measured by the violation itself.** `ش ه ر B001` identifies the calendrical month, `ح ر م B005/B006` gives it truce and inviolable claim, and `ق ص ص B003` imposes equivalent retaliation. The threefold `ع د و B001` traces crossing and counter-crossing, but `م ث ل B001` fixes the latter to a counterpart and `و ق ي B002` requires guarded self-placement. Following two cessation clauses in 2:192-193, I infer that reciprocity here is a ceiling: sacred status does not erase defense, yet it intensifies the demand not to exceed equivalence.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:195 related equivalent defense to material support, confirming that sacred reciprocity is one bounded component of collective endurance rather than an autonomous violence rule.

## 2:195 — وَأَنفِقُوا۟ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ وَلَا تُلْقُوا۟ بِأَيْدِيكُمْ إِلَى ٱلتَّهْلُكَةِ ۛ وَأَحْسِنُوٓا۟ ۛ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يُحِبُّ ٱلْمُحْسِنِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Material outflow sustains the common way, while withholding or misdirecting agency can cast the group into ruin.** `ن ف ق B002/B003` combines outgoing expenditure with an opening that has an exit, `س ب ل B001` supplies the extended way, and `ل ق ي B005` with `ي د ي B009` pictures agents casting by their own acts toward `ه ل ك B002`, self-incurred destruction. `ح س ن B002` then assigns skillful good action, reinforced by `ح ب ب B002`, as the counterpractice. Between bounded defense (2:190-194) and costly pilgrimage provisions (2:196), I infer that collective endurance requires resources to circulate toward the shared course rather than remain detached from it.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:196-197 made the circulation concrete through offerings, substitutes, provisions, and conduct needed to carry a community through pilgrimage.

## 2:196 — وَأَتِمُّوا۟ ٱلْحَجَّ وَٱلْعُمْرَةَ لِلَّهِ ۚ فَإِنْ أُحْصِرْتُمْ فَمَا ٱسْتَيْسَرَ مِنَ ٱلْهَدْىِ ۖ وَلَا تَحْلِقُوا۟ رُءُوسَكُمْ حَتَّىٰ يَبْلُغَ ٱلْهَدْىُ مَحِلَّهُۥ ۚ فَمَن كَانَ مِنكُم مَّرِيضًا أَوْ بِهِۦٓ أَذًۭى مِّن رَّأْسِهِۦ فَفِدْيَةٌۭ مِّن صِيَامٍ أَوْ صَدَقَةٍ أَوْ نُسُكٍۢ ۚ فَإِذَآ أَمِنتُمْ فَمَن تَمَتَّعَ بِٱلْعُمْرَةِ إِلَى ٱلْحَجِّ فَمَا ٱسْتَيْسَرَ مِنَ ٱلْهَدْىِ ۚ فَمَن لَّمْ يَجِدْ فَصِيَامُ ثَلَٰثَةِ أَيَّامٍۢ فِى ٱلْحَجِّ وَسَبْعَةٍ إِذَا رَجَعْتُمْ ۗ تِلْكَ عَشَرَةٌۭ كَامِلَةٌۭ ۗ ذَٰلِكَ لِمَن لَّمْ يَكُنْ أَهْلُهُۥ حَاضِرِى ٱلْمَسْجِدِ ٱلْحَرَامِ ۚ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ شَدِيدُ ٱلْعِقَابِ

### Activated readings

1. **Pilgrimage completion is preserved through a graded substitution system responsive to confinement, bodily harm, means, and residence.** `ت م م B001` sets completion as the objective of `ح ج ج B001`, directed going, while `ح ص ر B004` names enforced confinement and `ي س ر B001` selects what becomes feasible. `ه د ي B005` is the sanctuary offering, `ح ل ق B002` shaving waits until it `ب ل غ B001`, reaches, its `ح ل ل B002/B004`, settled due place; discomfort can instead trigger `ف د ي B001` through `ص و م B001` or `ن س ك B002/B003`. For one lacking what can be `و ج د B001/B003`, the counted fast reaches `ك م ل B001`, full completion, with residence near `ح ر م B003` differentiating the rule. I infer that ritual integrity lies in preserving a proportionate obligation across unequal conditions, not in forcing a single material performance after its enabling conditions fail.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:197-199 confirmed that ritual integrity exceeds one material performance: behavior, lawful livelihood, common movement, and repentance all participate in completion.

## 2:197 — ٱلْحَجُّ أَشْهُرٌۭ مَّعْلُومَٰتٌۭ ۚ فَمَن فَرَضَ فِيهِنَّ ٱلْحَجَّ فَلَا رَفَثَ وَلَا فُسُوقَ وَلَا جِدَالَ فِى ٱلْحَجِّ ۗ وَمَا تَفْعَلُوا۟ مِنْ خَيْرٍۢ يَعْلَمْهُ ٱللَّهُ ۗ وَتَزَوَّدُوا۟ فَإِنَّ خَيْرَ ٱلزَّادِ ٱلتَّقْوَىٰ ۚ وَٱتَّقُونِ يَٰٓأُو۟لِى ٱلْأَلْبَٰبِ

### Activated readings

1. **Entering the known pilgrimage interval converts travel into a bounded discipline of appetite, conduct, and speech.** `ح ج ج B001` is directed going, `ف ر ض B002` makes its undertaking a bounded determination, `ر ف ث B001/B002` supplies sexual speech and intercourse, and `ج د ل B001/B002` supplies twisted contention and dispute. The positive countercommand `ز و د B001` makes cautious placement, `و ق ي B002`, the true travel provision, addressed to `ل ب ب B003/B004`, the purified core or intellect. After 2:196's external substitutions and before 2:198's permitted livelihood-seeking, I infer that completion depends as much on governing relational behavior as on reaching ritual locations.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:198 confirmed that restraint does not require rejecting ordinary livelihood, while 2:199 made common movement and forgiveness the route's stronger discipline.

## 2:198 — لَيْسَ عَلَيْكُمْ جُنَاحٌ أَن تَبْتَغُوا۟ فَضْلًۭا مِّن رَّبِّكُمْ ۚ فَإِذَآ أَفَضْتُم مِّنْ عَرَفَٰتٍۢ فَٱذْكُرُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ عِندَ ٱلْمَشْعَرِ ٱلْحَرَامِ ۖ وَٱذْكُرُوهُ كَمَا هَدَىٰكُمْ وَإِن كُنتُم مِّن قَبْلِهِۦ لَمِنَ ٱلضَّآلِّينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Seeking material increase can remain inside pilgrimage when the collective flow is reoriented by remembrance at the sacred marker.** `ب غ ي B001` assigns seeking its ordinary role, while `ف ي ض B001/B002` makes departure from Arafat an easy, collective overflow. Repeated `ذ ك ر B003/B004` turns that movement toward recalled and spoken attention at `ش ع ر B006`, the ritual emblem, within `ح ر م B003`; `ه د ي B002` contrasts the given course with `ض ل ل B001`, loss of aim. I infer that commerce is not excluded from devotion but must be nested within a movement whose decisive coordinates come from guidance rather than gain.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:199-200 subordinated gain to common flow and intensified remembrance, confirming that commerce remains legitimate only within the pilgrimage's governing coordinates.

## 2:199 — ثُمَّ أَفِيضُوا۟ مِنْ حَيْثُ أَفَاضَ ٱلنَّاسُ وَٱسْتَغْفِرُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ غَفُورٌۭ رَّحِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **The commanded overflow joins the pilgrim to the people's route and pairs that leveling movement with a request for covering.** Repeated `ف ي ض B002` makes both clauses a collective rushing flow, and `غ ف ر B001/B002` makes the requested response protective covering and forgiveness. The “where” clause corrects any route that would distinguish a subgroup from the people, while 2:200 immediately redirects completed rites into intensified remembrance. I infer that a shared ritual path repairs status separation: one completes the movement by entering the common current, not by preserving a privileged departure.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:200 confirmed the leveling effect by redirecting completed common movement away from status memory and toward stronger remembrance of God.

## 2:200 — فَإِذَا قَضَيْتُم مَّنَٰسِكَكُمْ فَٱذْكُرُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ كَذِكْرِكُمْ ءَابَآءَكُمْ أَوْ أَشَدَّ ذِكْرًۭا ۗ فَمِنَ ٱلنَّاسِ مَن يَقُولُ رَبَّنَآ ءَاتِنَا فِى ٱلدُّنْيَا وَمَا لَهُۥ فِى ٱلْءَاخِرَةِ مِنْ خَلَٰقٍۢ

### Activated readings

1. **Completed rites transfer the intensity of ancestral recollection toward God and expose requests trapped in the nearer horizon.** `ق ض ي B004/B005` marks the rites' completed execution, `ن س ك B003` supplies those rites, and repeated `ذ ك ر B003/B004` assigns recall and voiced mention the post-ritual task. `ء ب و B001` evokes parental nurture as the comparison, while `د ن و B002` makes the requested world the nearer or lower sphere and `خ ل ق B005` denies the one-horizon petitioner a fitting share later. I infer that ritual completion is tested by what reorganizes memory and desire afterward, not merely by whether the sequence was performed.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:201 supplied the direct correction to near-world-only desire by asking for coherent good in both horizons; 2:202 then tied its share to accountable acquisition.

## 2:201 — وَمِنْهُم مَّن يَقُولُ رَبَّنَآ ءَاتِنَا فِى ٱلدُّنْيَا حَسَنَةًۭ وَفِى ٱلْءَاخِرَةِ حَسَنَةًۭ وَقِنَا عَذَابَ ٱلنَّارِ

### Activated readings

1. **The balanced petition refuses to trade the nearer good against the later good.** `د ن و B002` assigns the first horizon nearness rather than ultimacy, repeated `ح س ن B002` asks for skillfully realized good in both horizons, and `و ق ي B001` asks for a protective barrier from the fire's punishment. Set against 2:200's near-world-only request and followed by an earned share in 2:202, the doubled good changes the prayer from accumulation in one domain to coherence across both. I infer that a sound desire is tested by whether its immediate benefit remains compatible with its eventual outcome.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:202 tied the two-horizon request to an acquired share under rapid reckoning, and 2:203 placed all timing choices under one gathering.

## 2:202 — أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ لَهُمْ نَصِيبٌۭ مِّمَّا كَسَبُوا۟ ۚ وَٱللَّهُ سَرِيعُ ٱلْحِسَابِ

### Activated readings

1. **The petitioners' portion is tied to acquisition and brought rapidly under reckoning.** `ن ص ب B005` supplies an assigned share, `ك س ب B001` makes it arise from sought and obtained gain, `س ر ع B001` removes delay, and `ح س ب B001` assigns counting and reckoning as the governing process. The demonstrative points back to the two-horizon request of 2:201, while 2:203 closes the rites with gathering before God. I infer that prayer does not detach outcome from agency: what is requested is answered within an accountable economy of what one earns.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:203 confirmed that rapid reckoning culminates in common gathering, while 2:204-205 showed that pleasing speech cannot substitute for what conduct earns.

## 2:203 — ۞ وَٱذْكُرُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ فِىٓ أَيَّامٍۢ مَّعْدُودَٰتٍۢ ۚ فَمَن تَعَجَّلَ فِى يَوْمَيْنِ فَلَآ إِثْمَ عَلَيْهِ وَمَن تَأَخَّرَ فَلَآ إِثْمَ عَلَيْهِ ۚ لِمَنِ ٱتَّقَىٰ ۗ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّكُمْ إِلَيْهِ تُحْشَرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Different departure timings remain equivalent when counted remembrance is governed by guarded intent.** `ذ ك ر B003/B004` assigns recalled and voiced attention to `ع د د B003/B005`, prescribed counted days; `ع ج ل B001` brings one departure forward, yet neither timing carries `ء ث م B004`, loaded fault, for one practicing `و ق ي B002`, cautious self-placement. `ح ش ر B001` then places both routes under the same eventual gathering. I infer that calendrical variation is not moral variation when each option stays inside the stated boundary and common destination.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:204-207 shifted the audit from permitted timing to post-ritual character, confirming that guarded intent rather than calendrical variation distinguishes the actors.

## 2:204 — وَمِنَ ٱلنَّاسِ مَن يُعْجِبُكَ قَوْلُهُۥ فِى ٱلْحَيَوٰةِ ٱلدُّنْيَا وَيُشْهِدُ ٱللَّهَ عَلَىٰ مَا فِى قَلْبِهِۦ وَهُوَ أَلَدُّ ٱلْخِصَامِ

### Activated readings

1. **Impressive near-world speech can conceal a heart organized around extreme contention.** `ع ج ب B003` makes the utterance pleasing or impressive, `د ن و B002` limits its arena to the nearer life, and `ل د د B001` intensifies contentiousness inside `خ ص م B001`, adversarial dispute. The appeal to God as witness increases the speech's surface credibility, but 2:205 supplies the decisive after-turning evidence of corruption. I infer that the later ecological and generational effects reverse the initial reading: verbal appeal is not evidence of inward truth when the speaker's unsupervised course destroys what sustains life.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:205 supplied the decisive reversal by showing cultivation and progeny destroyed after the speaker turns away; 2:206 then exposed resistance to correction as the mechanism.

## 2:205 — وَإِذَا تَوَلَّىٰ سَعَىٰ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ لِيُفْسِدَ فِيهَا وَيُهْلِكَ ٱلْحَرْثَ وَٱلنَّسْلَ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ لَا يُحِبُّ ٱلْفَسَادَ

### Activated readings

1. **Corruption is diagnosed by purposeful activity that ruins both production and succession.** `س ع ي B001/B002` makes the movement intentional work, `ف س د B001` marks departure from sound balance, and `ه ل ك B001` assigns ruin to `ح ر ث B001/B002`, productive work and cultivation, and `ن س ل B001`, progeny in succession. The verse supplies the behavioral disclosure promised by 2:204's contrast between speech and heart; 2:206 then shows pride blocking correction. I infer that corruption is systemic rather than merely personal here because its paired objects are the means by which a society feeds itself and continues beyond itself.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:206 confirmed systemic corruption as feedback-resistant action, while 2:207 supplied the whole-self counterfigure whose agency is no longer organized around self-preservation.

## 2:206 — وَإِذَا قِيلَ لَهُ ٱتَّقِ ٱللَّهَ أَخَذَتْهُ ٱلْعِزَّةُ بِٱلْإِثْمِ ۚ فَحَسْبُهُۥ جَهَنَّمُ ۚ وَلَبِئْسَ ٱلْمِهَادُ

### Activated readings

1. **Honor becomes destructive when correction is seized as a reason to persist in fault.** `و ق ي B002` is the requested cautious repositioning, but `ع ز ز B001/B002` turns might and honor into an overcoming grip joined to `ء ث م B004`, loaded fault. `ح س ب B003` then declares the consequence sufficient, while `م ه د B002` makes the final spread place an anti-rest. The sequence from pleasing speech (2:204) through ruined cultivation (2:205) reaches its mechanism here. I infer that the core failure is correction-resistance: status is used to prevent the feedback that could interrupt corruption.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:207-208 gave the inverse of correction-resistance: the self is exchanged for approval and then enters peace wholly rather than defending status through fault.

## 2:207 — وَمِنَ ٱلنَّاسِ مَن يَشْرِى نَفْسَهُ ٱبْتِغَآءَ مَرْضَاتِ ٱللَّهِ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ رَءُوفٌۢ بِٱلْعِبَادِ

### Activated readings

1. **The counterfigure exchanges the self in pursuit of approval rather than exchanging truth for status.** `ش ر ي B001/B002` supplies exchange and equivalence, `ب غ ي B001` makes approval the pursued object, `ر ض و B002` names sought abundant approval, and `ر ء ف B001` closes with tender compassion toward servants. Positioned against 2:204-206's self-protective speaker and before total entry into peace in 2:208, the verse assigns costly availability to the alternative path. I infer that “selling the self” is a revaluation of agency: the self ceases to be the asset that every action must preserve.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:208 confirmed the revaluation by requiring total entry into peace; a self no longer treated as untouchable can commit without leaving reserved escape routes.

## 2:208 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ ٱدْخُلُوا۟ فِى ٱلسِّلْمِ كَآفَّةًۭ وَلَا تَتَّبِعُوا۟ خُطُوَٰتِ ٱلشَّيْطَٰنِ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ لَكُمْ عَدُوٌّۭ مُّبِينٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Peace requires total entry because the rival route advances through followable increments.** `د خ ل B001` makes peace an entered domain, `س ل م B001/B004` combines soundness with reconciliation, and `ك ف ف B006` gives the entry total inclusion. Against it, `ت ب ع B003` and `خ ط و B002` portray stepwise tracking of traces, while `ع د و B003` and `ب ي ن B004` identify a disclosed enemy. Following 2:207's whole-self exchange and preceding 2:209's possible slip, I infer that partial commitment leaves continuity points through which small tracked steps can redirect the whole course.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:209-210 confirmed the incremental model: tracked steps become a slip after clarification, and delayed response reaches a point where the affair is already closed.

## 2:209 — فَإِن زَلَلْتُم مِّنۢ بَعْدِ مَا جَآءَتْكُمُ ٱلْبَيِّنَٰتُ فَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَزِيزٌ حَكِيمٌ

### Activated readings

1. **A post-disclosure slip is not lack of orientation but departure from an already clarified footing.** `ز ل ل B001/B002` supplies slipping from place and error, `ب ي ن B004` makes the received signs clarifying, and `ع ز ز B001/B002` assigns might that cannot be overcome. The conditional follows the traced steps of 2:208 and precedes 2:210's completed affair. I infer that the warning concerns incremental loss of position after sufficient evidence, so responsibility lies in failing to hold the disclosed ground rather than in never having been shown it.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:210 made post-disclosure delay terminal, while 2:211's many clear signs confirmed that lack of evidence is not the cause of the slip.

## 2:210 — هَلْ يَنظُرُونَ إِلَّآ أَن يَأْتِيَهُمُ ٱللَّهُ فِى ظُلَلٍۢ مِّنَ ٱلْغَمَامِ وَٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ وَقُضِىَ ٱلْأَمْرُ ۚ وَإِلَى ٱللَّهِ تُرْجَعُ ٱلْأُمُورُ

### Activated readings

1. **Waiting for overwhelming manifestation postpones response until the affair has already reached completion.** `ظ ل ل B003` supplies overhead canopies, `غ م م B001/B002` covering cloud and obscured light, `ق ض ي B001/B004` a decisive ruling brought to completion, and `ر ج ع B001` the return of affairs. After clarified signs and possible slipping in 2:209, the expected spectacle is paired with closure rather than further deliberation. I infer that demanded certainty can function as delay: by the time disclosure is made irresistible, the open interval for choosing has ended.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:211-213 confirmed that waiting for irresistible certainty coexists with abundant signs; substitution and rivalry, not informational absence, block response.

## 2:211 — سَلْ بَنِىٓ إِسْرَٰٓءِيلَ كَمْ ءَاتَيْنَٰهُم مِّنْ ءَايَةٍۭ بَيِّنَةٍۢ ۗ وَمَن يُبَدِّلْ نِعْمَةَ ٱللَّهِ مِنۢ بَعْدِ مَا جَآءَتْهُ فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ شَدِيدُ ٱلْعِقَابِ

### Activated readings

1. **Repeated clear signs make later substitution of bestowed well-being an accountable conversion.** `ب ي ن B004` assigns the signs clarity, `ب د ل B001/B002` makes the response replacement and transformation, `ن ع م B001` identifies what is transformed as bestowed well-being, and `ع ق ب B007` gives the end a punitive consequence. The “after it came” pattern echoes the post-clarification slip in 2:209, while 2:213 shows clarified scripture becoming an object of rivalry. I infer that a favor is substituted when its received orienting function is redirected into an instrument of division or entitlement.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:212-213 showed bestowed favor being transformed into displayed rank and competitive ownership of revelation, strongly confirming the orienting-function inference.

## 2:212 — زُيِّنَ لِلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ ٱلْحَيَوٰةُ ٱلدُّنْيَا وَيَسْخَرُونَ مِنَ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ ۘ وَٱلَّذِينَ ٱتَّقَوْا۟ فَوْقَهُمْ يَوْمَ ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ يَرْزُقُ مَن يَشَآءُ بِغَيْرِ حِسَابٍۢ

### Activated readings

1. **Near-life adornment produces a temporary hierarchy of ridicule that provision later overturns.** `ز ي ن B002/B003` makes the nearer life visibly beautified, `س خ ر B003` assigns mockery toward believers, and `و ق ي B002` identifies the mocked group's guarded placement. `ر ز ق B001` then makes provision an allotted gift that exceeds `ح س ب B001`, ordinary counting. Between transformed favor in 2:211 and competitive disagreement in 2:213, I infer that ridicule mistakes displayed possession for rank; uncounted allotment exposes the instability of that metric.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:213 exposed the adorned hierarchy's instability inside revelation itself, where clarified truth becomes rivalry until guidance restores a common course.

## 2:213 — كَانَ ٱلنَّاسُ أُمَّةًۭ وَٰحِدَةًۭ فَبَعَثَ ٱللَّهُ ٱلنَّبِيِّۦنَ مُبَشِّرِينَ وَمُنذِرِينَ وَأَنزَلَ مَعَهُمُ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ بِٱلْحَقِّ لِيَحْكُمَ بَيْنَ ٱلنَّاسِ فِيمَا ٱخْتَلَفُوا۟ فِيهِ ۚ وَمَا ٱخْتَلَفَ فِيهِ إِلَّا ٱلَّذِينَ أُوتُوهُ مِنۢ بَعْدِ مَا جَآءَتْهُمُ ٱلْبَيِّنَٰتُ بَغْيًۢا بَيْنَهُمْ ۖ فَهَدَى ٱللَّهُ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ لِمَا ٱخْتَلَفُوا۟ فِيهِ مِنَ ٱلْحَقِّ بِإِذْنِهِۦ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ يَهْدِى مَن يَشَآءُ إِلَىٰ صِرَٰطٍۢ مُّسْتَقِيمٍ

### Activated readings

1. **Revelation adjudicates human divergence, yet possession of revelation can itself become the site of oppressive divergence.** `ء م م B004` supplies a community and `و ح د B005` unity in a shared respect; `ب ع ث B001/B002` rouses and dispatches `ن ب ء B003`, prophets, while `ن ذ ر B001` gives warning its cautioning role. `ك ت ب B001/B003` joins a binding text to `ح ق ق B002`, due truth, so `ح ك م B002` can adjudicate the ties marked by `ب ي ن B003`. Repeated `خ ل ف B004` shows people taking divergent ways, but the second divergence arises from `ب غ ي B003`, overstepping within those ties, after clarification; `ه د ي B002`, by authorization `ء ذ ن B004`, restores an intended course toward `ص ر ط B001`. I infer that the text does not mechanically eliminate conflict: it becomes guidance where rivalry no longer controls how its distinctions are received.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:214 confirmed that restored guidance does not remove ordeal, and 2:215 made material support part of carrying a community through that divergence.

## 2:214 — أَمْ حَسِبْتُمْ أَن تَدْخُلُوا۟ ٱلْجَنَّةَ وَلَمَّا يَأْتِكُم مَّثَلُ ٱلَّذِينَ خَلَوْا۟ مِن قَبْلِكُم ۖ مَّسَّتْهُمُ ٱلْبَأْسَآءُ وَٱلضَّرَّآءُ وَزُلْزِلُوا۟ حَتَّىٰ يَقُولَ ٱلرَّسُولُ وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ مَعَهُۥ مَتَىٰ نَصْرُ ٱللَّهِ ۗ أَلَآ إِنَّ نَصْرَ ٱللَّهِ قَرِيبٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Entry is corrected from an assumed entitlement into endurance through destabilizing precedent.** `ح س ب B002` marks the opening as supposition, `د خ ل B001` makes the garden an entry not yet attained, and `م ث ل B001` brings prior communities as a counterpart pattern. `ز ل ز ل B001/B002` assigns shaking disturbance and historical shocks so severe that messenger and believers ask when `ن ص ر B001`, prevailing aid, will arrive; `ق ر ب B001` answers with nearness. Following 2:213's guided path through dispute, I infer that right orientation does not predict an unshaken route; the extremity of the question can coexist with, rather than disprove, approaching aid.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:215-218 showed endurance's infrastructure: expenditure supports exposed relations, combat bears disliked cost, and migration plus striving refuse coerced return.

## 2:215 — يَسْـَٔلُونَكَ مَاذَا يُنفِقُونَ ۖ قُلْ مَآ أَنفَقْتُم مِّنْ خَيْرٍۢ فَلِلْوَٰلِدَيْنِ وَٱلْأَقْرَبِينَ وَٱلْيَتَٰمَىٰ وَٱلْمَسَٰكِينِ وَٱبْنِ ٱلسَّبِيلِ ۗ وَمَا تَفْعَلُوا۟ مِنْ خَيْرٍۢ فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ بِهِۦ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **The answer redirects attention from the substance expended to the relational route of its outflow.** Repeated `ن ف ق B002` makes wealth go out, `خ ي ر B005` assigns beneficence to that outflow, and the recipients map concentric vulnerability: `و ل د B002` parents, `ق ر ب B003` kinship, `ي ت م B001` offspring cut off from protection, `س ك ن B006` poverty, and `س ب ل B001` the extended way embodied by its traveler. Coming after communal ordeal in 2:214 and before costly combat in 2:216, I infer that expenditure builds the support network through which a community can endure demands without abandoning exposed members.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:216-218 confirmed that relational support prepares for costs beyond money, including reluctant defense, displacement, and strenuous capacity.

## 2:216 — كُتِبَ عَلَيْكُمُ ٱلْقِتَالُ وَهُوَ كُرْهٌۭ لَّكُمْ ۖ وَعَسَىٰٓ أَن تَكْرَهُوا۟ شَيْـًۭٔا وَهُوَ خَيْرٌۭ لَّكُمْ ۖ وَعَسَىٰٓ أَن تُحِبُّوا۟ شَيْـًۭٔا وَهُوَ شَرٌّۭ لَّكُمْ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ يَعْلَمُ وَأَنتُمْ لَا تَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Affective attraction and aversion are treated as insufficient measures of an action's actual outcome.** `ك ت ب B003` makes `ق ت ل B011`, mutual combat, a binding prescription, while `ك ر ه B001/B002` names aversion and unwillingly borne hardship. The chiastic contrast sets disliked `خ ي ر B005`, beneficence, against loved `ش ر ر B001`, badness, with `ح ب ب B002` supplying clinging attachment. The sacred-month comparison in 2:217 immediately constrains the prescription through competing harms. I infer that the verse suspends feeling as a final evaluator without erasing it: reluctant obedience must still be measured by disclosed moral relations, not by desire alone.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:217 supplied the needed moral comparison, ranking sacred violation within ongoing obstruction and coercion rather than letting aversion or attraction decide alone.

## 2:217 — يَسْـَٔلُونَكَ عَنِ ٱلشَّهْرِ ٱلْحَرَامِ قِتَالٍۢ فِيهِ ۖ قُلْ قِتَالٌۭ فِيهِ كَبِيرٌۭ ۖ وَصَدٌّ عَن سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ وَكُفْرٌۢ بِهِۦ وَٱلْمَسْجِدِ ٱلْحَرَامِ وَإِخْرَاجُ أَهْلِهِۦ مِنْهُ أَكْبَرُ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ ۚ وَٱلْفِتْنَةُ أَكْبَرُ مِنَ ٱلْقَتْلِ ۗ وَلَا يَزَالُونَ يُقَٰتِلُونَكُمْ حَتَّىٰ يَرُدُّوكُمْ عَن دِينِكُمْ إِنِ ٱسْتَطَٰعُوا۟ ۚ وَمَن يَرْتَدِدْ مِنكُمْ عَن دِينِهِۦ فَيَمُتْ وَهُوَ كَافِرٌۭ فَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ حَبِطَتْ أَعْمَٰلُهُمْ فِى ٱلدُّنْيَا وَٱلْءَاخِرَةِ ۖ وَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلنَّارِ ۖ هُمْ فِيهَا خَٰلِدُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Sacred-time combat is admitted as grave, then placed inside a hierarchy of graver sustained obstructions and forced reversals.** `ش ه ر B001` and `ح ر م B005/B006` establish calendrical truce and inviolability; `ق ت ل B001/B011` names killing and mutual combat, while `ص د د B001/B005` makes turning people from `س ب ل B001` a blocking barrier. `ف ت ن B003/B006` is diversion and coercive conflict, and repeated `ر د د B001/B005` makes the opponents' continuing aim return and religious reversion within `د ي ن B001/B006`. In the wake of 2:216's warning against affective judgment and before migration in 2:218, I infer that moral comparison here neither trivializes sacred violation nor isolates it from the ongoing coercive structure that produced the conflict.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:218 embodied the hierarchy of harms through migration and striving, confirming that the issue is sustained coercive reversal rather than an isolated calendrical breach.

## 2:218 — إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَٱلَّذِينَ هَاجَرُوا۟ وَجَٰهَدُوا۟ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ يَرْجُونَ رَحْمَتَ ٱللَّهِ ۚ وَٱللَّهُ غَفُورٌۭ رَّحِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Belief becomes expectant hope through severing migration and capacity spent under strain.** `ه ج ر B001/B002` combines separation with movement from one abode to another, `ج ه د B001` assigns strenuous expenditure of capacity, and `س ب ل B001` gives both acts a shared extended way. `ر ج و B001` makes mercy an expectant hope rather than a possessed entitlement, while `غ ف ر B002` supplies forgiveness. Following 2:217's expulsion and pressure to reverse religion, I infer that migration and striving are the embodied refusal of forced return; hope remains open precisely because these acts do not purchase mercy as a debt.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:219-220 related hope to circulation and vulnerable care, confirming that strenuous acts orient toward mercy without turning that mercy into purchased entitlement.

## 2:219 — ۞ يَسْـَٔلُونَكَ عَنِ ٱلْخَمْرِ وَٱلْمَيْسِرِ ۖ قُلْ فِيهِمَآ إِثْمٌۭ كَبِيرٌۭ وَمَنَٰفِعُ لِلنَّاسِ وَإِثْمُهُمَآ أَكْبَرُ مِن نَّفْعِهِمَا ۗ وَيَسْـَٔلُونَكَ مَاذَا يُنفِقُونَ قُلِ ٱلْعَفْوَ ۗ كَذَٰلِكَ يُبَيِّنُ ٱللَّهُ لَكُمُ ٱلْءَايَٰتِ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَتَفَكَّرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The judgment weighs acknowledged benefit against a larger obstructive load, then directs expendable surplus outward.** `خ م ر B001/B002` combines covering with intoxicating drink, and `ي س ر B007` names gambling lots and apportioned gain; both contain `ن ف ع B001`, benefit, yet their `ء ث م B001/B004`, slowing and loaded fault, is greater. The expenditure answer uses `ن ف ق B002` with `ع ف و B003`, easy surplus or remainder, while `ب ي ن B004` clarifies the signs so `ف ك ر B001` can turn the comparison over. I infer that the common issue is resource circulation: acquisitive practices whose social-moral drag exceeds benefit are countered by releasing what exceeds need.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:220-221 extended the resource comparison into fiduciary and household relations, confirming that benefit must be judged by the ties it repairs or directs.

## 2:220 — فِى ٱلدُّنْيَا وَٱلْءَاخِرَةِ ۗ وَيَسْـَٔلُونَكَ عَنِ ٱلْيَتَٰمَىٰ ۖ قُلْ إِصْلَاحٌۭ لَّهُمْ خَيْرٌۭ ۖ وَإِن تُخَالِطُوهُمْ فَإِخْوَٰنُكُمْ ۚ وَٱللَّهُ يَعْلَمُ ٱلْمُفْسِدَ مِنَ ٱلْمُصْلِحِ ۚ وَلَوْ شَآءَ ٱللَّهُ لَأَعْنَتَكُمْ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَزِيزٌ حَكِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Care for orphans is evaluated by restorative purpose within shared life, not by sterile separation.** `ي ت م B001` identifies those cut off from parental protection, `ص ل ح B002/B003` makes reconciliation and suitability their good, and `خ ل ط B001/B002` permits mixing as association and shared partnership. The contrast with `ف س د B001` makes intent and effect, rather than contact itself, the distinction; `ع ن ت B001` presents a stricter imposed arrangement as severe hardship. Following 2:219's command to release surplus and preceding household alignment in 2:221, I infer that fiduciary boundaries must protect the vulnerable while still allowing genuine kin-like incorporation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:221-222 confirmed that kin-like incorporation is not boundaryless: household direction and temporary bodily distance protect shared life from harmful mixing.

## 2:221 — وَلَا تَنكِحُوا۟ ٱلْمُشْرِكَٰتِ حَتَّىٰ يُؤْمِنَّ ۚ وَلَأَمَةٌۭ مُّؤْمِنَةٌ خَيْرٌۭ مِّن مُّشْرِكَةٍۢ وَلَوْ أَعْجَبَتْكُمْ ۗ وَلَا تُنكِحُوا۟ ٱلْمُشْرِكِينَ حَتَّىٰ يُؤْمِنُوا۟ ۚ وَلَعَبْدٌۭ مُّؤْمِنٌ خَيْرٌۭ مِّن مُّشْرِكٍۢ وَلَوْ أَعْجَبَكُمْ ۗ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ يَدْعُونَ إِلَى ٱلنَّارِ ۖ وَٱللَّهُ يَدْعُوٓا۟ إِلَى ٱلْجَنَّةِ وَٱلْمَغْفِرَةِ بِإِذْنِهِۦ ۖ وَيُبَيِّنُ ءَايَٰتِهِۦ لِلنَّاسِ لَعَلَّهُمْ يَتَذَكَّرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Household partnership is evaluated by the direction of its call rather than by attractiveness or social rank.** `ن ك ح B002/B003` gives marriage contract and the act of giving in marriage, while `ش ر ك B002/B003` overlays divine association with affinal partnership. `ء م و B001` makes the believing bondwoman the lower-status comparison, yet repeated `د ع و B001` reveals the decisive contrast between calls toward fire and toward the garden and `غ ف ر B002`, forgiveness, by `ء ذ ن B004`, authorization. `ب ي ن B004` and `ذ ك ر B003` make this directional distinction something disclosed for recollection. I infer that intimate alliance amplifies a household's governing orientation, so visible desirability cannot compensate for a call directed toward a contrary end.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:222-223 confirmed that household direction is embodied in temporary limits and forward cultivation, not merely stated affiliation or social rank.

## 2:222 — وَيَسْـَٔلُونَكَ عَنِ ٱلْمَحِيضِ ۖ قُلْ هُوَ أَذًۭى فَٱعْتَزِلُوا۟ ٱلنِّسَآءَ فِى ٱلْمَحِيضِ ۖ وَلَا تَقْرَبُوهُنَّ حَتَّىٰ يَطْهُرْنَ ۖ فَإِذَا تَطَهَّرْنَ فَأْتُوهُنَّ مِنْ حَيْثُ أَمَرَكُمُ ٱللَّهُ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يُحِبُّ ٱلتَّوَّٰبِينَ وَيُحِبُّ ٱلْمُتَطَهِّرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Temporary sexual distance is keyed to unsettled bodily discomfort and ends through a marked transition into purity.** `ح ي ض B001` identifies menstruation, `ء ذ ي B001` assigns disliked discomfort that cannot settle, `ع ز ل B001` requires setting aside, and `ق ر ب B001` bars approach within that interval. The two forms of `ط ه ر B002/B003/B005` distinguish post-menstrual purity, active washing, and cleansed conduct; repeated `ح ب ب B002` attaches approval to return and purification rather than to indefinite avoidance. Read between marriage alignment in 2:221 and cultivation in 2:223, I infer that the boundary protects embodied conditions while preserving the relationship beyond the condition's endpoint.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:223-224 showed the boundary's wider purpose: bodily return feeds accountable future provision, while no sacred formula may obstruct relational good.

## 2:223 — نِسَآؤُكُمْ حَرْثٌۭ لَّكُمْ فَأْتُوا۟ حَرْثَكُمْ أَنَّىٰ شِئْتُمْ ۖ وَقَدِّمُوا۟ لِأَنفُسِكُمْ ۚ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّكُم مُّلَٰقُوهُ ۗ وَبَشِّرِ ٱلْمُؤْمِنِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **The cultivation image places sexual choice inside productive consequence and forward provision.** `ح ر ث B001/B002` combines earned work with sowing and cultivation, `ق د م B004` commands beforeward advance, and `و ق ي B002` requires cautious self-placement under eventual encounter. Coming directly after the menstrual limit in 2:222 and before oath and divorce constraints in 2:224-229, the permissive phrase is surrounded by accountability rather than possession. I infer that “as you wish” varies approach within the named cultivated relation; it does not detach desire from what the partners sow for their future selves.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:224-228 surrounded sexual choice with heart-accountable oaths, finite waiting, resolved release, and reciprocal claims, strongly confirming the nonpossessive reading.

## 2:224 — وَلَا تَجْعَلُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ عُرْضَةًۭ لِّأَيْمَٰنِكُمْ أَن تَبَرُّوا۟ وَتَتَّقُوا۟ وَتُصْلِحُوا۟ بَيْنَ ٱلنَّاسِ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ سَمِيعٌ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **An oath invoking God must not become an interception across the path of relational good.** `ع ر ض B004/B011` supplies both interception and being set up for something, while `ي م ن B003` identifies the oath. Its blocked objects are `ب ر ر B001/B002`, conduct that holds and expansive righteousness, `و ق ي B002`, guarded placement, and `ص ل ح B002` within `ب ي ن B003`, reconciliation in human ties; `س م ع B003` makes the utterance understood rather than empty sound. The next ayah distinguishes non-counting speech from heart-earned commitment. I infer that divine naming cannot sanctify refusal to repair: the oath is judged by whether it obstructs the goods it claims to protect.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:225 distinguished settled commitment from verbal noise, and 2:226 converted a real oath into a finite process rather than letting it block repair indefinitely.

## 2:225 — لَّا يُؤَاخِذُكُمُ ٱللَّهُ بِٱللَّغْوِ فِىٓ أَيْمَٰنِكُمْ وَلَٰكِن يُؤَاخِذُكُم بِمَا كَسَبَتْ قُلُوبُكُمْ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ غَفُورٌ حَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Oath accountability follows inwardly acquired commitment rather than every uncounted utterance.** `ل غ و B001/B002` marks speech set aside as not counting or vain, `ي م ن B003` keeps the context oath-bound, and `ك س ب B001` assigns the heart an acquired act. `غ ف ر B002` and `ح ل م B001` add forgiveness and restraint rather than indiscriminate seizure. Following 2:224's warning against oath-based obstruction and preceding the four-month oath process in 2:226, I infer that legal-moral force arises from settled appropriation by the heart, not from accidental verbal surface alone.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:226-227 confirmed that inward acquisition has concrete temporal force: the oath opens a return interval and then a required fork into reconciliation or release.

## 2:226 — لِّلَّذِينَ يُؤْلُونَ مِن نِّسَآئِهِمْ تَرَبُّصُ أَرْبَعَةِ أَشْهُرٍۢ ۖ فَإِن فَآءُو فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ غَفُورٌۭ رَّحِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **A spousal abstention oath is given a finite watchful interval in which return remains possible.** `ء ل و B003` identifies swearing, `ر ب ص B001/B003` makes the response watchful marital waiting, and `ر ب ع B001` with `ش ه ر B001` fixes it at four months. `ف ي ء B001` then names turning back, answered by `غ ف ر B002`, forgiveness. The sequence converts 2:225's inward oath commitment into a timed household consequence, while 2:227 makes divorce the alternative firm course. I infer that the waiting period prevents an oath from suspending a spouse indefinitely: it opens bounded time for return and then demands resolution.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:227-228 confirmed that the four months cannot become limbo, because nonreturn moves into resolved divorce and a separately bounded waiting process.

## 2:227 — وَإِنْ عَزَمُوا۟ ٱلطَّلَٰقَ فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ سَمِيعٌ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **If return is not chosen, divorce must become a firm resolved release rather than prolonged ambiguity.** `ع ز م B001/B004` binds the heart to a chosen course, `ط ل ق B001/B002` lets loose from marital restraint, and `س م ع B003` makes the resolution understood and accountable.
