Its brevity completes the fork opened in 2:226, while 2:228-232 regulate what follows release. I infer that resolve here protects against indefinite relational limbo: the oath-holder must either turn back or accept the procedural consequences of ending the bond.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:228-232 expanded release into reciprocal rights, counted reversibility, broken automatic return, non-harm, and consensual remarriage, confirming the protective purpose of resolution.

## 2:228 — وَٱلْمُطَلَّقَٰتُ يَتَرَبَّصْنَ بِأَنفُسِهِنَّ ثَلَٰثَةَ قُرُوٓءٍۢ ۚ وَلَا يَحِلُّ لَهُنَّ أَن يَكْتُمْنَ مَا خَلَقَ ٱللَّهُ فِىٓ أَرْحَامِهِنَّ إِن كُنَّ يُؤْمِنَّ بِٱللَّهِ وَٱلْيَوْمِ ٱلْءَاخِرِ ۚ وَبُعُولَتُهُنَّ أَحَقُّ بِرَدِّهِنَّ فِى ذَٰلِكَ إِنْ أَرَادُوٓا۟ إِصْلَٰحًۭا ۚ وَلَهُنَّ مِثْلُ ٱلَّذِى عَلَيْهِنَّ بِٱلْمَعْرُوفِ ۚ وَلِلرِّجَالِ عَلَيْهِنَّ دَرَجَةٌۭ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ عَزِيزٌ حَكِيمٌ

### Activated readings

1. **The waiting interval preserves reproductive knowledge and a repair opportunity within reciprocal recognized claims.** `ط ل ق B002` supplies divorce, `ر ب ص B003` the marital waiting period, and `ق ر ء B003` the three menstrual-purity cycles; `ك ت م B001` forbids concealing what is in `ر ح م B003`, the womb. `ب ع ل B001` identifies the husband whose `ر د د B001` return is more due through `ح ق ق B002` only when `ص ل ح B002`, reconciliation, is intended. `م ث ل B001` makes women's recognized claims counterparts to obligations, while `د ر ج B003` adds a procedural step. In the next ayat that step is tightly bounded by counted returns, release, and non-harm; I infer that the local degree concerns the revocable interval's differentiated agency, not an unrestricted rank overriding reciprocity.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:229-232 strongly confined the local procedural degree through a two-return cap, a third-release barrier, explicit non-harm, and a ban on blocking mutual consent.

## 2:229 — ٱلطَّلَٰقُ مَرَّتَانِ ۖ فَإِمْسَاكٌۢ بِمَعْرُوفٍ أَوْ تَسْرِيحٌۢ بِإِحْسَٰنٍۢ ۗ وَلَا يَحِلُّ لَكُمْ أَن تَأْخُذُوا۟ مِمَّآ ءَاتَيْتُمُوهُنَّ شَيْـًٔا إِلَّآ أَن يَخَافَآ أَلَّا يُقِيمَا حُدُودَ ٱللَّهِ ۖ فَإِنْ خِفْتُمْ أَلَّا يُقِيمَا حُدُودَ ٱللَّهِ فَلَا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْهِمَا فِيمَا ٱفْتَدَتْ بِهِۦ ۗ تِلْكَ حُدُودُ ٱللَّهِ فَلَا تَعْتَدُوهَا ۚ وَمَن يَتَعَدَّ حُدُودَ ٱللَّهِ فَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ هُمُ ٱلظَّٰلِمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Revocable divorce is numerically capped and morally split between recognized retention and skillful release.** `ط ل ق B002` is counted through `م ر ر B002`, two occurrences; `م س ك B001` makes one option holding, while `س ر ح B001/B002` makes the other unhindered marital release, qualified by `ح س ن B002`. Repeated `ح د د B001/B002` turns the divine bounds into barring edges, with `ف د ي B001` permitting the wife's compensatory release where the pair fears they cannot uphold them; `ع د و B001` forbids crossing those edges. I infer that neither return nor transferred property may be used to trap a failing bond: bounded reversibility exists to test repair, not to multiply leverage.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:230-232 confirmed bounded reversibility by breaking automatic return, invalidating harmful retention, and protecting consensual remarriage from third-party control.

## 2:230 — فَإِن طَلَّقَهَا فَلَا تَحِلُّ لَهُۥ مِنۢ بَعْدُ حَتَّىٰ تَنكِحَ زَوْجًا غَيْرَهُۥ ۗ فَإِن طَلَّقَهَا فَلَا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْهِمَآ أَن يَتَرَاجَعَآ إِن ظَنَّآ أَن يُقِيمَا حُدُودَ ٱللَّهِ ۗ وَتِلْكَ حُدُودُ ٱللَّهِ يُبَيِّنُهَا لِقَوْمٍۢ يَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **A third release breaks automatic return and makes any later reunion pass through a genuinely independent marriage history.** `ط ل ق B001/B002` again lets the bond loose, `ن ك ح B002/B004` requires marriage contract and spousal attachment to another, and only after that bond also ends can `ر ج ع B001` name mutual return. Repeated `ح د د B001/B002` and `ب ي ن B004` make the rule a clarified barring edge. Following the two-return cap in 2:229 and preceding the anti-harm command in 2:231, I infer that the intervening bond prevents serial divorce from functioning as a reversible threat controlled by the first husband.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:231 supplied the direct anti-threat rule: formally holding a bond for harm is itself transgression, confirming why the intervening marriage must be independent.

## 2:231 — وَإِذَا طَلَّقْتُمُ ٱلنِّسَآءَ فَبَلَغْنَ أَجَلَهُنَّ فَأَمْسِكُوهُنَّ بِمَعْرُوفٍ أَوْ سَرِّحُوهُنَّ بِمَعْرُوفٍۢ ۚ وَلَا تُمْسِكُوهُنَّ ضِرَارًۭا لِّتَعْتَدُوا۟ ۚ وَمَن يَفْعَلْ ذَٰلِكَ فَقَدْ ظَلَمَ نَفْسَهُۥ ۚ وَلَا تَتَّخِذُوٓا۟ ءَايَٰتِ ٱللَّهِ هُزُوًۭا ۚ وَٱذْكُرُوا۟ نِعْمَتَ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْكُمْ وَمَآ أَنزَلَ عَلَيْكُم مِّنَ ٱلْكِتَٰبِ وَٱلْحِكْمَةِ يَعِظُكُم بِهِۦ ۚ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ بِكُلِّ شَىْءٍ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Term-end retention is invalid when its operative purpose is reciprocal harm and boundary-crossing.** `ب ل غ B001` marks arrival at the term, `م س ك B001` permits holding only within recognized good, and `س ر ح B001/B002` offers unhindered release on the same basis. `ض ر ر B002` exposes intentional or reciprocal harm, joined to `ع د و B001`, overstepping; `ه ز ء B001` then names treating the signs as mockery, while `و ع ظ B001` restores their warning function. I infer that formal use of a permitted option can still negate the rule when its purpose is to injure, making procedural compliance without recognized good a misuse of revelation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:232-233 generalized purpose over form, barring protective authority when it blocks consent and requiring consultation where a child's care continues.

## 2:232 — وَإِذَا طَلَّقْتُمُ ٱلنِّسَآءَ فَبَلَغْنَ أَجَلَهُنَّ فَلَا تَعْضُلُوهُنَّ أَن يَنكِحْنَ أَزْوَٰجَهُنَّ إِذَا تَرَٰضَوْا۟ بَيْنَهُم بِٱلْمَعْرُوفِ ۗ ذَٰلِكَ يُوعَظُ بِهِۦ مَن كَانَ مِنكُمْ يُؤْمِنُ بِٱللَّهِ وَٱلْيَوْمِ ٱلْءَاخِرِ ۗ ذَٰلِكُمْ أَزْكَىٰ لَكُمْ وَأَطْهَرُ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ يَعْلَمُ وَأَنتُمْ لَا تَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Once the term is reached, third parties may not convert protection into restraint that blocks consensual remarriage.** `ع ض ل B004` names severe restraint blocking a right, `ن ك ح B002` the marriage contract, and `ر ض و B003` mutual consent located in `ب ي ن B003`, the parties' tie, under recognized good. `و ع ظ B001` frames the prohibition as admonition, while `ز ك و B001/B002` and `ط ه ر B005` make noninterference more growth-producing and conduct-cleansing. After 2:231 rejects harmful retention and before 2:233 requires parental consultation, I infer that communal guardianship loses legitimacy when it displaces the informed agreement of the people whose bond is at issue.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:233 confirmed informed agreement as a recurring household principle through mutual consent, consultation, capacity, and non-harm in infant care.

## 2:233 — ۞ وَٱلْوَٰلِدَٰتُ يُرْضِعْنَ أَوْلَٰدَهُنَّ حَوْلَيْنِ كَامِلَيْنِ ۖ لِمَنْ أَرَادَ أَن يُتِمَّ ٱلرَّضَاعَةَ ۚ وَعَلَى ٱلْمَوْلُودِ لَهُۥ رِزْقُهُنَّ وَكِسْوَتُهُنَّ بِٱلْمَعْرُوفِ ۚ لَا تُكَلَّفُ نَفْسٌ إِلَّا وُسْعَهَا ۚ لَا تُضَآرَّ وَٰلِدَةٌۢ بِوَلَدِهَا وَلَا مَوْلُودٌۭ لَّهُۥ بِوَلَدِهِۦ ۚ وَعَلَى ٱلْوَارِثِ مِثْلُ ذَٰلِكَ ۗ فَإِنْ أَرَادَا فِصَالًا عَن تَرَاضٍۢ مِّنْهُمَا وَتَشَاوُرٍۢ فَلَا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْهِمَا ۗ وَإِنْ أَرَدتُّمْ أَن تَسْتَرْضِعُوٓا۟ أَوْلَٰدَكُمْ فَلَا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْكُمْ إِذَا سَلَّمْتُم مَّآ ءَاتَيْتُم بِٱلْمَعْرُوفِ ۗ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ بَصِيرٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Post-separation infant care distributes nurture, material support, and decision-making without allowing the child to become leverage.** `ر ض ع B001/B002` makes nursing both feeding and a kinship-forming act across two complete `ح و ل B002`, year-cycles, while `ر ز ق B001` and `ك س و B001` assign provision and clothing. `ك ل ف B003` is bounded by `و س ع B002`, capacity; repeated `ض ر ر B002` bars reciprocal harm through the child, and `و ر ث B001` carries support to the heir. Early `ف ص ل B003`, separation from nursing, requires `ر ض و B003`, mutual consent, and `ش و ر B003`, drawn-out counsel; payment must be handed over through `س ل م B012`. I infer that the child's continuity reorganizes former spouses into a cooperative care structure whose limits are capacity, non-harm, and consultation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:234-237 extended the cooperative structure into widow transition and proportioned divorce support, confirming that changed bonds leave continuing duties rather than leverage.

## 2:234 — وَٱلَّذِينَ يُتَوَفَّوْنَ مِنكُمْ وَيَذَرُونَ أَزْوَٰجًۭا يَتَرَبَّصْنَ بِأَنفُسِهِنَّ أَرْبَعَةَ أَشْهُرٍۢ وَعَشْرًۭا ۖ فَإِذَا بَلَغْنَ أَجَلَهُنَّ فَلَا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْكُمْ فِيمَا فَعَلْنَ فِىٓ أَنفُسِهِنَّ بِٱلْمَعْرُوفِ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ خَبِيرٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Death creates a fixed transition interval, after which the widow's recognized self-directed action is released from communal liability.** `و ف ي B002` receives the deceased soul in full, `و ذ ر B003` marks the spouse left, and `ر ب ص B003` sets marital waiting for `ر ب ع B001` months plus `ع ش ر B001` days. Once `ب ل غ B001` reaches the term, recognized action carries no offense, under `خ ب ر B001`, tested inner knowledge. With 2:235 barring secret contracts before the term and 2:240 securing year-long provision without forced residence, I infer that waiting protects transition but does not transfer permanent control over the widow's future to the community.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:235 protected the interval from secret pre-completion, while 2:236-237 ensured that its eventual end does not erase measured economic consequences.

## 2:235 — وَلَا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْكُمْ فِيمَا عَرَّضْتُم بِهِۦ مِنْ خِطْبَةِ ٱلنِّسَآءِ أَوْ أَكْنَنتُمْ فِىٓ أَنفُسِكُمْ ۚ عَلِمَ ٱللَّهُ أَنَّكُمْ سَتَذْكُرُونَهُنَّ وَلَٰكِن لَّا تُوَاعِدُوهُنَّ سِرًّا إِلَّآ أَن تَقُولُوا۟ قَوْلًۭا مَّعْرُوفًۭا ۚ وَلَا تَعْزِمُوا۟ عُقْدَةَ ٱلنِّكَاحِ حَتَّىٰ يَبْلُغَ ٱلْكِتَٰبُ أَجَلَهُۥ ۚ وَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ يَعْلَمُ مَا فِىٓ أَنفُسِكُمْ فَٱحْذَرُوهُ ۚ وَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ غَفُورٌ حَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Interest may appear indirectly during waiting, but a binding marital knot cannot be privately pre-completed.** `ع ر ض B010` permits indirect wording and `خ ط ب B002` identifies proposal, while `ك ن ن B002` allows inward concealment. `و ع د B004` bars secret mutual appointment through `س ر ر B001`; only recognized speech may precede the point where `ع ز م B001` could form `ع ق د B001/B002`, a fastening and ratified obligation, in `ن ك ح B002`. `ب ل غ B001` and `ك ت ب B003` make the prescribed term decisive, with `ح ذ ر B001` and `ح ل م B001` joining caution to forbearance. I infer that the rule accommodates real anticipation while preventing secrecy from hollowing out the widow's protected interval.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:236-237 confirmed that anticipation may be acknowledged without hollowing out the term, because any resulting bond still carries proportioned or fixed material obligations.

## 2:236 — لَّا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْكُمْ إِن طَلَّقْتُمُ ٱلنِّسَآءَ مَا لَمْ تَمَسُّوهُنَّ أَوْ تَفْرِضُوا۟ لَهُنَّ فَرِيضَةًۭ ۚ وَمَتِّعُوهُنَّ عَلَى ٱلْمُوسِعِ قَدَرُهُۥ وَعَلَى ٱلْمُقْتِرِ قَدَرُهُۥ مَتَٰعًۢا بِٱلْمَعْرُوفِ ۖ حَقًّا عَلَى ٱلْمُحْسِنِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Even a marriage ended before contact or assigned dower creates a due transition benefit proportioned to means.** `ط ل ق B002` releases the marriage before `م س س B002`, sexual contact, or `ف ر ض B002/B003`, bounded assigned grant. Repeated `م ت ع B001/B003` makes compensation usable benefit and provision; `و س ع B003` distinguishes ample means from `ق ت ر B001`, narrowing, while `ق د ر B001/B006` calibrates each to fitting measure. `ح ق ق B002` and `ح س ن B002` make this a due of skillful good action under recognized practice. I infer that absence of consummation cancels neither dignity nor economic consequence; proportionality replaces a nonexistent fixed amount.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:237 refined proportionality: a fixed grant creates a half-share floor, while voluntary release and remembered bounty may exceed exact entitlement.

## 2:237 — وَإِن طَلَّقْتُمُوهُنَّ مِن قَبْلِ أَن تَمَسُّوهُنَّ وَقَدْ فَرَضْتُمْ لَهُنَّ فَرِيضَةًۭ فَنِصْفُ مَا فَرَضْتُمْ إِلَّآ أَن يَعْفُونَ أَوْ يَعْفُوَا۟ ٱلَّذِى بِيَدِهِۦ عُقْدَةُ ٱلنِّكَاحِ ۚ وَأَن تَعْفُوٓا۟ أَقْرَبُ لِلتَّقْوَىٰ ۚ وَلَا تَنسَوُا۟ ٱلْفَضْلَ بَيْنَكُمْ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ بَصِيرٌ

### Activated readings

1. **A fixed pre-contact dower is halved by rule, then reopened toward voluntary release and remembered generosity.** `م س س B002` again marks absent sexual contact, `ف ر ض B003` the assigned grant, and `ن ص ف B001` its half. `ع ف و B001/B003` allows either wiping the claim or releasing an easy remainder around `ع ق د B001/B002`, the marriage knot; this is `ق ر ب B001` to `و ق ي B002`, guarded placement. `ن س ي B001/B002` bars forgetting or abandoning `ف ض ل B001/B003` within `ب ي ن B003`, the tie between them. I infer that exact entitlement is a floor for just closure, not a command to suppress voluntary beneficence after the bond changes.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:238 placed prayer at the center of exact entitlement and voluntary generosity, and 2:240-241 continued transition provision beyond the immediate calculation.

## 2:238 — حَٰفِظُوا۟ عَلَى ٱلصَّلَوَٰتِ وَٱلصَّلَوٰةِ ٱلْوُسْطَىٰ وَقُومُوا۟ لِلَّهِ قَٰنِتِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **The prayer command places steady worship at the balancing center of a dense field of household claims.** `ح ف ظ B001/B003` requires preservation and steady observance of `ص ل و B003`, prescribed worship; `و س ط B001/B002` singles out just balance at the middle, and `ق ن ت B001/B004` makes standing obedient and prayerfully silent. Its insertion between divorce compensation (2:236-237) and threat-adjusted worship (2:239) interrupts legal detail with recurring orientation. I infer that prayer functions structurally as a stabilizing center: rights and concessions are practiced by agents repeatedly recalled to obedient standing beyond their immediate interests.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:239 confirmed that the orienting center survives altered posture under danger; 2:240 then returned that orientation to ordinary material protection.

## 2:239 — فَإِنْ خِفْتُمْ فَرِجَالًا أَوْ رُكْبَانًۭا ۖ فَإِذَآ أَمِنتُمْ فَٱذْكُرُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ كَمَا عَلَّمَكُم مَّا لَمْ تَكُونُوا۟ تَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Danger changes prayer's posture without canceling its orienting obligation, and safety restores explicit recollection.** `ر ك ب B001` supplies mounted movement as the alternative to being on foot, while `ذ ك ر B003/B004` assigns recalled and voiced attention after security and learned knowledge. The conditional directly modifies 2:238's preserved standing, and 2:240 returns to ordinary household provision. I infer that stable form serves remembrance but is not identical with it: when threat removes the normal posture, mobile observance preserves the relation until fuller form is again possible.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:240-242 showed mobile worship returning into widow and divorce provision, confirming that adaptable form preserves rather than suspends accountable relations.

## 2:240 — وَٱلَّذِينَ يُتَوَفَّوْنَ مِنكُمْ وَيَذَرُونَ أَزْوَٰجًۭا وَصِيَّةًۭ لِّأَزْوَٰجِهِم مَّتَٰعًا إِلَى ٱلْحَوْلِ غَيْرَ إِخْرَاجٍۢ ۚ فَإِنْ خَرَجْنَ فَلَا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْكُمْ فِى مَا فَعَلْنَ فِىٓ أَنفُسِهِنَّ مِن مَّعْرُوفٍۢ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ عَزِيزٌ حَكِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **A deceased husband's charge secures a full year of usable support without converting support into compulsory residence.** `و ف ي B002` marks death, `و ذ ر B003` the spouse left behind, and `و ص ي B001/B002` an attached charge. `م ت ع B001/B003` gives that charge benefit and provision through a complete `ح و ل B002`, year-cycle, explicitly without expulsion; if the women leave, their recognized self-directed conduct incurs no offense. Read with the shorter transition interval in 2:234 and the general divorced provision in 2:241, I infer that maintenance and agency coexist: housing protection remains available, but the beneficiary is not imprisoned by the benefit.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:241 generalized recognized provision to divorced women and 2:242 framed the rules as tethering understanding, confirming support without compulsory residence.

## 2:241 — وَلِلْمُطَلَّقَٰتِ مَتَٰعٌۢ بِٱلْمَعْرُوفِ ۖ حَقًّا عَلَى ٱلْمُتَّقِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Recognized transition support is generalized from special divorce cases into a due practice of guarded people.** `ط ل ق B002` identifies the released women, `م ت ع B001/B003` gives them usable benefit and provision, `ع ر ف B005` subjects it to recognized good, and `ح ق ق B002` makes it due upon those practicing `و ق ي B002`, cautious self-placement. Coming after the widow's year provision in 2:240 and before rational clarification in 2:242, I infer that material closure is not discretionary kindness added after a valid release; it is part of what keeps release itself within an accountable social form.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:242 made provision a rationally tethered due, while 2:243-245 placed material release beside life, risk, and return rather than optional sentiment.

## 2:242 — كَذَٰلِكَ يُبَيِّنُ ٱللَّهُ لَكُمْ ءَايَٰتِهِۦ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَعْقِلُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The clarified household limits are meant to become restraints internalized through understanding.** `ب ي ن B004/B005` makes the signs clear and disclosed, while `ع ق ل B001/B002` joins understanding to tethering restraint. The demonstrative gathers the detailed provisions of 2:224-241, and 2:243 immediately tests whether fear-driven flight can evade the limits of life and death. I infer that legal detail reaches its purpose when agents can hold themselves back by grasping the relations the rules protect, not merely when they can repeat the boundaries.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:243 supplied a direct test of internalized restraint: mass fear seeks escape from death, then 2:244-245 require bodily and material exposure on an accountable way.

## 2:243 — ۞ أَلَمْ تَرَ إِلَى ٱلَّذِينَ خَرَجُوا۟ مِن دِيَٰرِهِمْ وَهُمْ أُلُوفٌ حَذَرَ ٱلْمَوْتِ فَقَالَ لَهُمُ ٱللَّهُ مُوتُوا۟ ثُمَّ أَحْيَٰهُمْ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ لَذُو فَضْلٍ عَلَى ٱلنَّاسِ وَلَٰكِنَّ أَكْثَرَ ٱلنَّاسِ لَا يَشْكُرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Mass flight from death is shown unable to secure vitality, while restored life appears as unearned bounty.** `د و ر B007` locates the thousands outside their residences, `ء ل ف B001` marks their gathered numerical scale, and `ح ذ ر B001` makes departure wary guarding against `م و ت B001`, loss of vitality. Their later animation is `ح ي ي B003/B013`, living existence and saving good, framed by `ف ض ل B003`, bounty, whose sustaining effect most fail to show through `ش ك ر B002`. Before the combat command of 2:244, I infer that fear-management cannot make survival sovereign; action must be governed by a larger account of gifted life than mere avoidance of risk.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:244-246 confirmed that gifted life cannot be secured by flight alone; defense and expenditure expose whether stated willingness survives actual prescription.

## 2:244 — وَقَٰتِلُوا۟ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ وَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ سَمِيعٌ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **The combat command answers fear-driven flight by relocating risk on an accountable way.** `ق ت ل B011` assigns mutual combat, `س ب ل B001` gives it an extended course, and `س م ع B003` makes what agents say in response understood and compliance-bearing. The command follows 2:243's failed attempt to preserve life by departure and precedes the call for a repayable good advance in 2:245, so bodily and material exposure are paired. I infer that the issue is not preference for danger but refusal to let self-preservation alone determine whether the shared way will be defended.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:245 paired bodily risk with a cut-off material advance, and 2:246 showed why heard readiness must still be tested when obligation becomes real.

## 2:245 — مَّن ذَا ٱلَّذِى يُقْرِضُ ٱللَّهَ قَرْضًا حَسَنًۭا فَيُضَٰعِفَهُۥ لَهُۥٓ أَضْعَافًۭا كَثِيرَةًۭ ۚ وَٱللَّهُ يَقْبِضُ وَيَبْصُۜطُ وَإِلَيْهِ تُرْجَعُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Voluntary expenditure is pictured as a cut-off advance whose return belongs to a larger cycle of contraction and expansion.** `ق ر ض B001/B003/B005` combines cutting off, a repayable advance, and reciprocal return; `ح س ن B002` qualifies it as skillful good, while `ض ع ف B002` and `ك ث ر B001` multiply equal amounts abundantly. `ق ب ض B006` contracts and `ب س ط B001/B003` extends with ample capacity before `ر ج ع B001`, return. Between the combat demand in 2:244 and the tested army in 2:246-249, I infer that releasing resources is an act of trust in a circulation not controlled by the giver's present possession.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:246-249 made circulation's trust concrete: claimed readiness, disputed leadership, inherited signs, and appetite restraint all test whether resources can be released from present control.

## 2:246 — أَلَمْ تَرَ إِلَى ٱلْمَلَإِ مِنۢ بَنِىٓ إِسْرَٰٓءِيلَ مِنۢ بَعْدِ مُوسَىٰٓ إِذْ قَالُوا۟ لِنَبِىٍّۢ لَّهُمُ ٱبْعَثْ لَنَا مَلِكًۭا نُّقَٰتِلْ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ ۖ قَالَ هَلْ عَسَيْتُمْ إِن كُتِبَ عَلَيْكُمُ ٱلْقِتَالُ أَلَّا تُقَٰتِلُوا۟ ۖ قَالُوا۟ وَمَا لَنَآ أَلَّا نُقَٰتِلَ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ وَقَدْ أُخْرِجْنَا مِن دِيَٰرِنَا وَأَبْنَآئِنَا ۖ فَلَمَّا كُتِبَ عَلَيْهِمُ ٱلْقِتَالُ تَوَلَّوْا۟ إِلَّا قَلِيلًۭا مِّنْهُمْ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ عَلِيمٌۢ بِٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Declared readiness under dispossession is exposed when the requested combat becomes binding rather than imagined.** `م ل ء B002` identifies the assembled elite, `ب ع ث B002` their request to dispatch a `م ل ك B003`, king, and `ق ت ل B011` their proposed mutual combat on `س ب ل B001`. Once `ك ت ب B003` makes that combat prescribed, `و ل ي B007` marks withdrawal by all but `ق ل ل B001`, a few. Their appeal to lost residences and children explains urgency but does not predict endurance. I infer that hypothetical zeal can borrow force from grievance; actual obligation distinguishes desire for restored status from willingness to bear its cost.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:247-249 confirmed that grievance-powered zeal is insufficient, because the same group contests selection by wealth and largely fails a simple restraint trial.

## 2:247 — وَقَالَ لَهُمْ نَبِيُّهُمْ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ قَدْ بَعَثَ لَكُمْ طَالُوتَ مَلِكًۭا ۚ قَالُوٓا۟ أَنَّىٰ يَكُونُ لَهُ ٱلْمُلْكُ عَلَيْنَا وَنَحْنُ أَحَقُّ بِٱلْمُلْكِ مِنْهُ وَلَمْ يُؤْتَ سَعَةًۭ مِّنَ ٱلْمَالِ ۚ قَالَ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ ٱصْطَفَىٰهُ عَلَيْكُمْ وَزَادَهُۥ بَسْطَةًۭ فِى ٱلْعِلْمِ وَٱلْجِسْمِ ۖ وَٱللَّهُ يُؤْتِى مُلْكَهُۥ مَن يَشَآءُ ۚ وَٱللَّهُ وَٰسِعٌ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Leadership selection overturns the elite's wealth-based claim by privileging chosen knowledge and embodied capacity.** Repeated `م ل ك B003` makes kingship the contested office, while `ح ق ق B002` voices the elite's alleged due and `و س ع B003` with `م و ل B001` their material criterion. `ص ف و B002` instead marks selection of the choice part, `ز ي د B001` added growth, `ب س ط B003` ample extension, and `ج س م B001/B002` perceptible bodily adequacy. The river trial in 2:249 then tests whether the selected force can restrain itself. I infer that legitimate capacity is task-shaped and disclosed through knowledge, body, and testing, not inferred from prior possession.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:248-251 validated task-shaped criteria through a continuity sign, appetite testing, stable footing, and eventual victory under a differently emerging leader.

## 2:248 — وَقَالَ لَهُمْ نَبِيُّهُمْ إِنَّ ءَايَةَ مُلْكِهِۦٓ أَن يَأْتِيَكُمُ ٱلتَّابُوتُ فِيهِ سَكِينَةٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّكُمْ وَبَقِيَّةٌۭ مِّمَّا تَرَكَ ءَالُ مُوسَىٰ وَءَالُ هَٰرُونَ تَحْمِلُهُ ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ ۚ إِنَّ فِى ذَٰلِكَ لَءَايَةًۭ لَّكُمْ إِن كُنتُم مُّؤْمِنِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **The sign of new kingship arrives as carried continuity: settled calm joined to a residue from earlier prophetic households.** `م ل ك B003` remains the questioned authority, `س ك ن B001/B004` supplies cessation of disturbance and an object of rest, and `ب ق ي B002` makes the contents a remaining portion. `ت ر ك B002/B006` gives that residue a persisting effect left behind, `ء و ل B003` identifies the households, and `ح م ل B001` makes the chest an outwardly borne load. Between capability selection in 2:247 and appetite testing in 2:249, I infer that the sign legitimates change by carrying trusted memory into it, without reducing authority to hereditary wealth.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:249-251 confirmed that carried memory legitimates but does not replace testing: restraint and steadiness still determine who can bear the inherited continuity.

## 2:249 — فَلَمَّا فَصَلَ طَالُوتُ بِٱلْجُنُودِ قَالَ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ مُبْتَلِيكُم بِنَهَرٍۢ فَمَن شَرِبَ مِنْهُ فَلَيْسَ مِنِّى وَمَن لَّمْ يَطْعَمْهُ فَإِنَّهُۥ مِنِّىٓ إِلَّا مَنِ ٱغْتَرَفَ غُرْفَةًۢ بِيَدِهِۦ ۚ فَشَرِبُوا۟ مِنْهُ إِلَّا قَلِيلًۭا مِّنْهُمْ ۚ فَلَمَّا جَاوَزَهُۥ هُوَ وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ مَعَهُۥ قَالُوا۟ لَا طَاقَةَ لَنَا ٱلْيَوْمَ بِجَالُوتَ وَجُنُودِهِۦ ۚ قَالَ ٱلَّذِينَ يَظُنُّونَ أَنَّهُم مُّلَٰقُوا۟ ٱللَّهِ كَم مِّن فِئَةٍۢ قَلِيلَةٍ غَلَبَتْ فِئَةً كَثِيرَةًۢ بِإِذْنِ ٱللَّهِ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ مَعَ ٱلصَّٰبِرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **The river discloses whether a gathered force can limit immediate appetite before measuring itself against superior numbers.** `ف ص ل B010` sends the army out, `ج ن د B001` makes it a gathered force, and `ب ل و B002` assigns the river trial disclosure of state. `ن ه ر B001` is flowing water, `ش ر ب B001` unrestricted drinking, and `غ ر ف B001` the permitted single lifting; after `ج و ز B002`, crossing, the survivors question `ط و ق B002`, capacity. `ف ء ي B003` contrasts separated groups, `ق ل ل B001` with `ك ث ر B001`, while `غ ل ب B001` by `ء ذ ن B004` and `ص ب ر B001` detach outcome from size alone. I infer that restraint is battlefield preparation: those unable to govern abundance at the river cannot reliably govern force under pressure.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:250-251 converted the river's restraint into prayed-for footing and authorized victory, strongly confirming self-government as preparation for collective force.

## 2:250 — وَلَمَّا بَرَزُوا۟ لِجَالُوتَ وَجُنُودِهِۦ قَالُوا۟ رَبَّنَآ أَفْرِغْ عَلَيْنَا صَبْرًۭا وَثَبِّتْ أَقْدَامَنَا وَٱنصُرْنَا عَلَى ٱلْقَوْمِ ٱلْكَٰفِرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Once exposed before the opposing force, the tested group asks for inner restraint to be poured out as stable outward footing.** `ب ر ز B001/B003` makes them appear and come out for combat, and `ف ر غ B002` pictures `ص ب ر B001`, self-restraint from panic, poured over them. `ث ب ت B003` assigns firming to `ق د م B001`, their stepping feet, before `ن ص ر B001`, aid that makes prevail. The prayer converts 2:249's general claim about patient minorities into requested bodily steadiness, answered by routing in 2:251. I infer that courage is not presumed possession but a supplied continuity between restrained affect and non-retreating action.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:251 answered the prayer through routing and then generalized victory into social counter-pressure; 2:252 preserved that full sequence as the recited claim.

## 2:251 — فَهَزَمُوهُم بِإِذْنِ ٱللَّهِ وَقَتَلَ دَاوُۥدُ جَالُوتَ وَءَاتَىٰهُ ٱللَّهُ ٱلْمُلْكَ وَٱلْحِكْمَةَ وَعَلَّمَهُۥ مِمَّا يَشَآءُ ۗ وَلَوْلَا دَفْعُ ٱللَّهِ ٱلنَّاسَ بَعْضَهُم بِبَعْضٍۢ لَّفَسَدَتِ ٱلْأَرْضُ وَلَٰكِنَّ ٱللَّهَ ذُو فَضْلٍ عَلَى ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **The particular victory is generalized into a social ecology in which human groups ward one another from unchecked corruption.** `ه ز م B002` routes the force, `ق ت ل B001` marks David's killing of Goliath, and `م ل ك B003` gives David kingship. The explanatory counterfactual uses `د ف ع B001` for pushing away and `ب ع ض B001` for people partitioned into portions; without that counter-pressure, `ف س د B001` would carry the earth out of sound balance. `ف ض ل B003` makes the arrangement bounty beyond the victors. I infer that power's constructive role is not unlimited dominance but prevention of monopoly through authorized counterforce.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:252 confirmed that victory must remain inside the sequence of withdrawal, selection, testing, and prayer; 2:253 warned that clear gifts still do not eliminate later agency.

## 2:252 — تِلْكَ ءَايَٰتُ ٱللَّهِ نَتْلُوهَا عَلَيْكَ بِٱلْحَقِّ ۚ وَإِنَّكَ لَمِنَ ٱلْمُرْسَلِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **The leadership and battle account is presented as sequenced recitation carrying a due truth to a messenger.** `ت ل و B001` makes the signs follow in sequence, `ح ق ق B002` gives the recitation due truth, and `ر س ل B001/B002` locates the recipient among those sent forth with message. The demonstrative gathers 2:246-251, while 2:253 immediately differentiates messengers and records later conflict. I infer that narrative sequence itself bears the claim: withdrawal, selection, restraint, victory, and counterforce must remain joined rather than reduced to a free-standing triumph scene.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:253 strengthened the sequencing requirement by differentiating messenger gifts and recording later conflict, preventing the battle account from becoming a simple triumph template.

## 2:253 — ۞ تِلْكَ ٱلرُّسُلُ فَضَّلْنَا بَعْضَهُمْ عَلَىٰ بَعْضٍۢ ۘ مِّنْهُم مَّن كَلَّمَ ٱللَّهُ ۖ وَرَفَعَ بَعْضَهُمْ دَرَجَٰتٍۢ ۚ وَءَاتَيْنَا عِيسَى ٱبْنَ مَرْيَمَ ٱلْبَيِّنَٰتِ وَأَيَّدْنَٰهُ بِرُوحِ ٱلْقُدُسِ ۗ وَلَوْ شَآءَ ٱللَّهُ مَا ٱقْتَتَلَ ٱلَّذِينَ مِنۢ بَعْدِهِم مِّنۢ بَعْدِ مَا جَآءَتْهُمُ ٱلْبَيِّنَٰتُ وَلَٰكِنِ ٱخْتَلَفُوا۟ فَمِنْهُم مَّنْ ءَامَنَ وَمِنْهُم مَّن كَفَرَ ۚ وَلَوْ شَآءَ ٱللَّهُ مَا ٱقْتَتَلُوا۟ وَلَٰكِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يَفْعَلُ مَا يُرِيدُ

### Activated readings

1. **Differentiated messenger gifts do not mechanically prevent later communities from turning clear signs into conflict.** `ر س ل B002` identifies the messengers, `ف ض ل B002` ranks some through virtue, `ك ل م B001` gives exchanged divine speech, and `ر ف ع B002` with `د ر ج B003` supplies raised ranks and steps. Jesus receives `ء ي د B001/B002`, reinforcing strength and protection, through `ر و ح B001` joined to `ق د س B001`, sanctifying purity. Yet `ق ت ل B011` occurs after clear signs because `خ ل ف B004` makes successors take divergent ways; repeated divine willing and `ر و د B001` frame this as permitted history, not divine inability. I infer that bestowed distinction creates responsibility but does not erase human agency or guarantee communal unanimity.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:254-258 confirmed that distinction creates present responsibility under dependent sovereignty: expenditure, authorized intercession, noncoercion, patronage, and contested kingship all retain human agency.

## 2:254 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوٓا۟ أَنفِقُوا۟ مِمَّا رَزَقْنَٰكُم مِّن قَبْلِ أَن يَأْتِىَ يَوْمٌۭ لَّا بَيْعٌۭ فِيهِ وَلَا خُلَّةٌۭ وَلَا شَفَٰعَةٌۭ ۗ وَٱلْكَٰفِرُونَ هُمُ ٱلظَّٰلِمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Provision must be released while exchange remains possible, before the day when relational substitutes cannot alter the account.** `ن ف ق B002` sends out what `ر ز ق B001` allotted as gift; the later day removes `ب ي ع B001`, priced exchange, `خ ل ل B003`, intimate friendship, and `ش ف ع B001/B002`, a joined advocate or helper. Coming after communities that fought despite clear signs in 2:253 and before authorized intercession in 2:255, the triad denies autonomous rescue mechanisms rather than all relation. I infer that present expenditure is irreplaceable action: neither assets nor allies can retroactively perform it once the accountable interval closes.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:255 clarified that intercession is not absent but authorized, confirming that the denied substitutes were autonomous rescue mechanisms rather than every joined relation.

## 2:255 — ٱللَّهُ لَآ إِلَٰهَ إِلَّا هُوَ ٱلْحَىُّ ٱلْقَيُّومُ ۚ لَا تَأْخُذُهُۥ سِنَةٌۭ وَلَا نَوْمٌۭ ۚ لَّهُۥ مَا فِى ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَمَا فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ ۗ مَن ذَا ٱلَّذِى يَشْفَعُ عِندَهُۥٓ إِلَّا بِإِذْنِهِۦ ۚ يَعْلَمُ مَا بَيْنَ أَيْدِيهِمْ وَمَا خَلْفَهُمْ ۖ وَلَا يُحِيطُونَ بِشَىْءٍۢ مِّنْ عِلْمِهِۦٓ إِلَّا بِمَا شَآءَ ۚ وَسِعَ كُرْسِيُّهُ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضَ ۖ وَلَا يَـُٔودُهُۥ حِفْظُهُمَا ۚ وَهُوَ ٱلْعَلِىُّ ٱلْعَظِيمُ

### Activated readings

1. **Exclusive divine life is presented as unbroken guardianship whose knowledge, authorization, and preservation cannot be independently occupied.** `ح ي ي B003` gives living being, while `ق و م B004/B009` assigns guardianship and sustaining mainstay; neither `و س ن B001`, drowsiness, nor `ن و م B001`, sleep, interrupts it. `ش ف ع B002` can join an advocate only by `ء ذ ن B004`, authorization; `ح و ط B004` denies creatures complete grasp of knowledge, while `و س ع B001`, spacious extent, and `ك ر س B001/B002`, compact fixed base, encompass both realms. Their `ح ف ظ B001` is no `ء و د B002`, bending burden, to the one marked by `ع ل و B001` and `ع ظ م B001`, height and greatness. I infer that the surrounding ayat's kingship, combat, and intercession are relativized: every delegated power remains dependent, wakefully held, and informationally bounded.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:256-257 confirmed sovereignty without coercive imitation: allegiance forms through a clarified firm grip, and authority is known by the direction it carries dependents.

## 2:256 — لَآ إِكْرَاهَ فِى ٱلدِّينِ ۖ قَد تَّبَيَّنَ ٱلرُّشْدُ مِنَ ٱلْغَىِّ ۚ فَمَن يَكْفُرْ بِٱلطَّٰغُوتِ وَيُؤْمِنۢ بِٱللَّهِ فَقَدِ ٱسْتَمْسَكَ بِٱلْعُرْوَةِ ٱلْوُثْقَىٰ لَا ٱنفِصَامَ لَهَا ۗ وَٱللَّهُ سَمِيعٌ عَلِيمٌ

### Activated readings

1. **Religious adherence is grounded in clarified distinction and a chosen firm grip, making coercion structurally contrary to the described bond.** `ك ر ه B003` names forcing another into what is disliked, within `د ي ن B001/B006`, obedience and ordered authority. `ب ي ن B004` distinguishes `ر ش د B001/B002`, straight sound judgment, from `غ و ي B001/B002`, dark straying and its ruin. Rejecting `ط غ ي B003`, false authority, leads to `م س ك B001`, holding, an `ع ر و B001/B002`, exposed handhold or precinct, made firm by `و ث ق B002`; `ف ص م B001` says it does not fissure apart. After 2:255's exhaustive divine sovereignty, I infer that God does not need coerced assent: durable allegiance is the intelligible grip that follows disclosed alternatives.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:257 made the noncoerced grip visibly directional, while 2:258-260 supplied argumentative and enacted disclosures rather than forced assent.

## 2:257 — ٱللَّهُ وَلِىُّ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ يُخْرِجُهُم مِّنَ ٱلظُّلُمَٰتِ إِلَى ٱلنُّورِ ۖ وَٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوٓا۟ أَوْلِيَآؤُهُمُ ٱلطَّٰغُوتُ يُخْرِجُونَهُم مِّنَ ٱلنُّورِ إِلَى ٱلظُّلُمَٰتِ ۗ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلنَّارِ ۖ هُمْ فِيهَا خَٰلِدُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Patronage is diagnosed by the direction in which it moves its dependents across light and darkness.** `و ل ي B003/B004` combines assuming charge with loyal support, `ن و ر B001` supplies illumination, and `ط غ ي B003` identifies false authorities. Their opposed movements end in `ص ح ب B001`, close association, with burning `ن و ر B002`, and `خ ل د B001/B002`, enduring permanence and clinging stay. The contrast concretizes 2:256's choice between God and false authority, while 2:258 shows kingship producing argumentative rebellion. I infer that authority's label is less diagnostic than its trajectory: true guardianship carries outward into intelligibility, whereas false guardianship reverses that movement.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:258 confirmed the trajectory test when received kingship becomes rebellion, while 2:259-260 showed guidance moving toward clarified and settled understanding.

## 2:258 — أَلَمْ تَرَ إِلَى ٱلَّذِى حَآجَّ إِبْرَٰهِۦمَ فِى رَبِّهِۦٓ أَنْ ءَاتَىٰهُ ٱللَّهُ ٱلْمُلْكَ إِذْ قَالَ إِبْرَٰهِۦمُ رَبِّىَ ٱلَّذِى يُحْىِۦ وَيُمِيتُ قَالَ أَنَا۠ أُحْىِۦ وَأُمِيتُ ۖ قَالَ إِبْرَٰهِۦمُ فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يَأْتِى بِٱلشَّمْسِ مِنَ ٱلْمَشْرِقِ فَأْتِ بِهَا مِنَ ٱلْمَغْرِبِ فَبُهِتَ ٱلَّذِى كَفَرَ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ لَا يَهْدِى ٱلْقَوْمَ ٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Abraham defeats a ruler's verbal imitation of life-and-death power by shifting proof from controllable subjects to cosmic regularity.** `ح ج ج B003` makes the exchange proof-contestation, provoked by received `م ل ك B003`, dominion. `ح ي ي B003/B013` and `م و ت B001` name living or saving good against loss of life; the ruler claims both, but Abraham points to `ش م س B001`, the sun, rising through `ش ر ق B001` and challenges its arrival from `غ ر ب B006`, the west. `ب ه ت B001` registers stunned bewilderment, and `ه د ي B002` is withheld from the wrongdoers' course. I infer that the second argument exposes equivocation: local power over persons does not equal authorship of the stable system within which that power acts.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:259-260 confirmed the scale correction by displaying restoration through reassembled remains and distributed parts, not through a ruler's local control over subjects.

## 2:259 — أَوْ كَٱلَّذِى مَرَّ عَلَىٰ قَرْيَةٍۢ وَهِىَ خَاوِيَةٌ عَلَىٰ عُرُوشِهَا قَالَ أَنَّىٰ يُحْىِۦ هَٰذِهِ ٱللَّهُ بَعْدَ مَوْتِهَا ۖ فَأَمَاتَهُ ٱللَّهُ مِا۟ئَةَ عَامٍۢ ثُمَّ بَعَثَهُۥ ۖ قَالَ كَمْ لَبِثْتَ ۖ قَالَ لَبِثْتُ يَوْمًا أَوْ بَعْضَ يَوْمٍۢ ۖ قَالَ بَل لَّبِثْتَ مِا۟ئَةَ عَامٍۢ فَٱنظُرْ إِلَىٰ طَعَامِكَ وَشَرَابِكَ لَمْ يَتَسَنَّهْ ۖ وَٱنظُرْ إِلَىٰ حِمَارِكَ وَلِنَجْعَلَكَ ءَايَةًۭ لِّلنَّاسِ ۖ وَٱنظُرْ إِلَى ٱلْعِظَامِ كَيْفَ نُنشِزُهَا ثُمَّ نَكْسُوهَا لَحْمًۭا ۚ فَلَمَّا تَبَيَّنَ لَهُۥ قَالَ أَعْلَمُ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ قَدِيرٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Restoration is disclosed through deliberately asynchronous signs: preserved provisions, elapsed subjective time, and visibly reassembled remains.** `م ر ر B001` carries the traveler past a `ق ر ي B001`, gathered settlement, marked by `خ و ي B001/B002`, vacancy and collapse, beneath `ع ر ش B002`, roofs. `م و ت B001` and `ب ع ث B001` bracket one hundred `ع و م B002`, year-cycles, although `ل ب ث B001` feels brief. Food and `ش ر ب B001`, drink, remain, while `ن ش ز B001/B002` raises bones and `ك س و B002` covers them with `ل ح م B001/B005`, flesh that joins; `ب ي ن B004` makes the contrast clear and `ق د ر B003` names effective capacity. I infer that the mismatched rates rule out ordinary continuity and make reconstitution, not mere survival, the evidentiary center.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:260 turned reconstitution into a repeatable compositional image for the heart, and 2:261 linked released units to structured multiplication rather than mere preservation.

## 2:260 — وَإِذْ قَالَ إِبْرَٰهِۦمُ رَبِّ أَرِنِى كَيْفَ تُحْىِ ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ ۖ قَالَ أَوَلَمْ تُؤْمِن ۖ قَالَ بَلَىٰ وَلَٰكِن لِّيَطْمَئِنَّ قَلْبِى ۖ قَالَ فَخُذْ أَرْبَعَةًۭ مِّنَ ٱلطَّيْرِ فَصُرْهُنَّ إِلَيْكَ ثُمَّ ٱجْعَلْ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ جَبَلٍۢ مِّنْهُنَّ جُزْءًۭا ثُمَّ ٱدْعُهُنَّ يَأْتِينَكَ سَعْيًۭا ۚ وَٱعْلَمْ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَزِيزٌ حَكِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Belief can seek enacted understanding of “how” so that the heart moves from assent into settled calm.** `ح ي ي B003` and `م و ت B001` state the object of inquiry, while `ط م ن B001` assigns its purpose calm settlement rather than reversal of belief. `ر ب ع B001` birds, `ط ي ر B001`, are turned toward Abraham through `ص و ر B001`, distributed as `ج ز ء B002`, portions, on each `ج ب ل B001`, high solid mass; `د ع و B001` then calls them near and `س ع ي B001` makes their return purposeful movement. I infer that the procedure turns abstract possibility into witnessed retrieval across separation, giving the heart a compositional image of restored life.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:261-263 carried retrieval into generative transfer: distributed wealth multiplies only when the giver does not attach ownership, injury, or domination after it.

## 2:261 — مَّثَلُ ٱلَّذِينَ يُنفِقُونَ أَمْوَٰلَهُمْ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ كَمَثَلِ حَبَّةٍ أَنۢبَتَتْ سَبْعَ سَنَابِلَ فِى كُلِّ سُنۢبُلَةٍۢ مِّا۟ئَةُ حَبَّةٍۢ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ يُضَٰعِفُ لِمَن يَشَآءُ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ وَٰسِعٌ عَلِيمٌ

### Activated readings

1. **Expenditure on the shared way is modeled as a carried kernel whose release generates structured, repeated abundance.** `م ث ل B001` establishes the counterpart, `ن ف ق B002` sends out `م و ل B001`, wealth, on `س ب ل B001`; `ح ب ب B001` supplies the seed-kernel, `ن ب ت B001` its emerging growth, `س ب ع B001` seven, and `س ن ب ل B001` grain ears holding a hundred each. `ض ع ف B002` adds equal amounts while `و س ع B003` keeps divine means ample. Following 2:260's gathered return of distributed parts and preceding the prohibition on injurious follow-up in 2:262, I infer that multiplication depends on a transfer remaining generative after leaving the giver, not on nominal loss alone.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:262-266 tests the seed image by showing that follow-up harm can sever growth, rooted intention yields under varied input, and nullified good becomes catastrophic when dependents need its fruit.

## 2:262 — ٱلَّذِينَ يُنفِقُونَ أَمْوَٰلَهُمْ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ ثُمَّ لَا يُتْبِعُونَ مَآ أَنفَقُوا۟ مَنًّۭا وَلَآ أَذًۭى ۙ لَّهُمْ أَجْرُهُمْ عِندَ رَبِّهِمْ وَلَا خَوْفٌ عَلَيْهِمْ وَلَا هُمْ يَحْزَنُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **A gift remains fruitful only when the giver does not send a diminishing claim or injury after it.** `ن ف ق B002` releases wealth, but `ت ب ع B001/B003` can attach a following trace; `م ن ن B001/B003` makes that trace either cut off the extension or place a weighty remembered favor on the recipient, and `ء ذ ي B001` adds unsettled harm. Without those attachments, `ء ج ر B001` supplies recompense and `ح ز ن B001` is removed. The “then” makes later conduct part of the original expenditure's evaluation, clarified further by 2:263-264. I infer that ownership can be covertly reasserted after transfer through social debt, nullifying the gift's intended release.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:263-264 confirms that subsequent conduct remains part of the gift: harmful follow-up is worse than a kind refusal and can render charity void.

## 2:263 — ۞ قَوْلٌۭ مَّعْرُوفٌۭ وَمَغْفِرَةٌ خَيْرٌۭ مِّن صَدَقَةٍۢ يَتْبَعُهَآ أَذًۭى ۗ وَٱللَّهُ غَنِىٌّ حَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Recognized speech and protective pardon can preserve more good than a material gift that carries injury behind it.** `ع ر ف B005` qualifies the utterance as recognized good, `غ ف ر B001/B002` gives covering and forgiveness, and `ص د ق B006` makes charity a relinquished right. When `ت ب ع B001` lets `ء ذ ي B001`, unsettled harm, follow it, the nonmaterial response becomes `خ ي ر B005`, more beneficent; `غ ن ي B001` and `ح ل م B001` remove divine need and add restraint. I infer that material magnitude does not compensate for relational degradation: declining or answering well may leave the recipient less harmed than giving with domination.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:264 confirms that relational degradation can nullify material transfer, while 2:265 supplies the positively rooted alternative.

## 2:264 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ لَا تُبْطِلُوا۟ صَدَقَٰتِكُم بِٱلْمَنِّ وَٱلْأَذَىٰ كَٱلَّذِى يُنفِقُ مَالَهُۥ رِئَآءَ ٱلنَّاسِ وَلَا يُؤْمِنُ بِٱللَّهِ وَٱلْيَوْمِ ٱلْءَاخِرِ ۖ فَمَثَلُهُۥ كَمَثَلِ صَفْوَانٍ عَلَيْهِ تُرَابٌۭ فَأَصَابَهُۥ وَابِلٌۭ فَتَرَكَهُۥ صَلْدًۭا ۖ لَّا يَقْدِرُونَ عَلَىٰ شَىْءٍۢ مِّمَّا كَسَبُوا۟ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ لَا يَهْدِى ٱلْقَوْمَ ٱلْكَٰفِرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Display-oriented charity has the appearance of cultivable soil but no depth in which a yield can remain.** `ب ط ل B001` makes reminder and harm render `ص د ق B006`, charity, void; `م ن ن B003` weighs the favor on another, `ء ذ ي B001` injures, and `ر ء ي B005` makes expenditure a show before people. `ص ف و B006` is a smooth stone carrying `ت ر ب B001`, dust, until `ص و ب B001` and `و ب ل B001`, descending heavy rain, leave `ص ل د B001/B002`, hard barren surface. `ق د ر B003` then denies effective control over what was `ك س ب B001`, acquired. I infer that public appearance can imitate fertile giving, but pressure reveals whether the act has rooted intention or only a removable layer.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:265 directly supplies the rooted-garden inverse to the washed stone, and 2:266 shows the delayed catastrophe of losing the expected yield.

## 2:265 — وَمَثَلُ ٱلَّذِينَ يُنفِقُونَ أَمْوَٰلَهُمُ ٱبْتِغَآءَ مَرْضَاتِ ٱللَّهِ وَتَثْبِيتًۭا مِّنْ أَنفُسِهِمْ كَمَثَلِ جَنَّةٍۭ بِرَبْوَةٍ أَصَابَهَا وَابِلٌۭ فَـَٔاتَتْ أُكُلَهَا ضِعْفَيْنِ فَإِن لَّمْ يُصِبْهَا وَابِلٌۭ فَطَلٌّۭ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ بَصِيرٌ

### Activated readings

1. **Approval-seeking expenditure is rooted by inner stabilization, so both heavy and slight provision produce yield.** `ن ف ق B002` sends wealth out, `ب غ ي B001` pursues `ر ض و B002`, sought approval, and `ث ب ت B002/B003` assigns steadiness originating from the selves. The counterpart garden stands on `ر ب و B002`, raised ground; `و ب ل B001` heavy rain produces `ض ع ف B002`, a doubled amount, yet `ط ل ل B001`, fine dew, also suffices. Contrasted with 2:264's thin dust and extended by 2:266's vulnerable garden, I infer that durable intention is a receptive structure: productivity does not depend on spectacular external input because the underlying relation can hold what arrives.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:266 shows why durable rooting matters when old age and weak dependents expose reliance on accumulated fruit, while 2:267 adds the quality of what is planted or transferred.

## 2:266 — أَيَوَدُّ أَحَدُكُمْ أَن تَكُونَ لَهُۥ جَنَّةٌۭ مِّن نَّخِيلٍۢ وَأَعْنَابٍۢ تَجْرِى مِن تَحْتِهَا ٱلْأَنْهَٰرُ لَهُۥ فِيهَا مِن كُلِّ ٱلثَّمَرَٰتِ وَأَصَابَهُ ٱلْكِبَرُ وَلَهُۥ ذُرِّيَّةٌۭ ضُعَفَآءُ فَأَصَابَهَآ إِعْصَارٌۭ فِيهِ نَارٌۭ فَٱحْتَرَقَتْ ۗ كَذَٰلِكَ يُبَيِّنُ ٱللَّهُ لَكُمُ ٱلْءَايَٰتِ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَتَفَكَّرُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The ruined garden makes delayed dependence the test of whether prior good has been preserved or nullified.** `و د د B002` asks whether anyone would wish for the scene; `ن خ ل B001`, palms, `ع ن ب B001`, vines, flowing `ج ر ي B001`, and `ث م ر B001/B003`, fruit and benefit, establish total productivity. Then `ك ب ر B004`, old age, coincides with weak progeny, `ض ع ف B001`; `ع ص ر B004` brings a whirlwind containing burning `ن و ر B002`, and `ح ر ق B002` consumes the garden. Following 2:262-265's warnings about post-gift nullification, I infer that injurious giving is catastrophic precisely when accumulated acts are most needed to support a future self and dependents who cannot replace them.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:267-268 shifts from destroyed yield to the quality of provision and the scarcity forecast behind withholding, confirming that future dependence should motivate good input rather than base disposal.

## 2:267 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوٓا۟ أَنفِقُوا۟ مِن طَيِّبَٰتِ مَا كَسَبْتُمْ وَمِمَّآ أَخْرَجْنَا لَكُم مِّنَ ٱلْأَرْضِ ۖ وَلَا تَيَمَّمُوا۟ ٱلْخَبِيثَ مِنْهُ تُنفِقُونَ وَلَسْتُم بِـَٔاخِذِيهِ إِلَّآ أَن تُغْمِضُوا۟ فِيهِ ۚ وَٱعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ غَنِىٌّ حَمِيدٌ

### Activated readings

1. **The quality of giving is tested by reciprocal acceptability: do not deliberately transfer what you would take only by overlooking its defect.** `ن ف ق B002` releases from `ط ي ب B001/B002`, good pure and untainted acquisitions, whereas `ي م م B001` deliberately aims at `خ ب ث B001`, base unwholesomeness. `غ م ض B004` names accepting it only through concession; `غ ن ي B001` makes God self-sufficient and `ح م د B001` praiseworthy, removing any notion that divine need validates refuse. After the garden images and before poverty's threat in 2:268, I infer that generosity cannot be measured by disposal cost to the giver while ignoring usability and dignity for the receiver.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:268 identifies threatened poverty as the pressure behind aiming at base goods, and 2:269 makes wisdom the faculty that adjudicates that pressure.

## 2:268 — ٱلشَّيْطَٰنُ يَعِدُكُمُ ٱلْفَقْرَ وَيَأْمُرُكُم بِٱلْفَحْشَآءِ ۖ وَٱللَّهُ يَعِدُكُم مَّغْفِرَةًۭ مِّنْهُ وَفَضْلًۭا ۗ وَٱللَّهُ وَٰسِعٌ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Giving is contested by rival forecasts: threatened poverty narrows action, while promised forgiveness and bounty reopen it.** `و ع د B002` makes the first forecast a threat, `ف ق ر B002` material need, and `ف ح ش B001/B002/B005` gross boundary-excess that can include miserly excess. The second `و ع د B001` opens expectation toward `غ ف ر B002`, forgiveness, and `ف ض ل B003`, bounty, under `و س ع B003`, ample means. Between the quality command in 2:267 and wisdom in 2:269, I infer that withholding begins as an imaginative economy: whichever promised future is trusted determines whether present resources close or circulate.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:269 confirms that choosing between rival projected futures requires wisdom, while 2:270 makes the resulting use or promised use of resources accountable.

## 2:269 — يُؤْتِى ٱلْحِكْمَةَ مَن يَشَآءُ ۚ وَمَن يُؤْتَ ٱلْحِكْمَةَ فَقَدْ أُوتِىَ خَيْرًۭا كَثِيرًۭا ۗ وَمَا يَذَّكَّرُ إِلَّآ أُو۟لُوا۟ ٱلْأَلْبَٰبِ

### Activated readings

1. **Wisdom is abundant good because it restrains and sets judgment right amid competing promises and uses of wealth.** `ح ك م B001/B003` combines restraining correction with rightly guided knowledge, `خ ي ر B005` makes it beneficence, and `ك ث ر B001` makes that good abundant. `ذ ك ر B003` is available to `ل ب ب B003/B004`, the pure core and purified intellect. Placed between scarcity-versus-bounty forecasts in 2:268 and accountable spending and vows in 2:270, I infer that wisdom is practical discrimination: it identifies which projected future should govern a resource decision.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:270-273 operationalizes wisdom through accountable spending and vows, calibrated visibility, recipient autonomy, and recognition of quiet need.

## 2:270 — وَمَآ أَنفَقْتُم مِّن نَّفَقَةٍ أَوْ نَذَرْتُم مِّن نَّذْرٍۢ فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يَعْلَمُهُۥ ۗ وَمَا لِلظَّٰلِمِينَ مِنْ أَنصَارٍ

### Activated readings

1. **Both completed outflow and self-bound future outflow remain fully known, leaving oppressive withholding without rescue.** Repeated `ن ف ق B002` covers wealth that has gone out, while `ن ذ ر B002` makes a vow a voluntarily bound obligation; `ن ص ر B001` denies aid that would make wrongdoers prevail. Following wisdom's resource judgment in 2:269 and preceding public-versus-hidden charity in 2:271, the pairing spans actual and promised giving. I infer that intention does not replace performance, but a vow is already accountable because it reorganizes what the speaker has declared available.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:271 confirms that public and hidden modes are both known, while 2:272 shows that giving returns to the giver without control over the recipient's guidance.

## 2:271 — إِن تُبْدُوا۟ ٱلصَّدَقَٰتِ فَنِعِمَّا هِىَ ۖ وَإِن تُخْفُوهَا وَتُؤْتُوهَا ٱلْفُقَرَآءَ فَهُوَ خَيْرٌۭ لَّكُمْ ۚ وَيُكَفِّرُ عَنكُم مِّن سَيِّـَٔاتِكُمْ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ خَبِيرٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Public charity remains good, but concealed delivery to actual poverty better protects the act from display and the recipient from exposure.** `ب د و B001` makes `ص د ق B006`, relinquished charity, visibly emerge, while `خ ف ي B001` covers it and `ف ق ر B002` identifies material need. The hidden route is `خ ي ر B005`, more beneficent, and removes some `س و ء B001`, badness, under `خ ب ر B001`, tested inner knowledge. Read after the display warning of 2:264 and before guidance is removed from the giver's control in 2:272, I infer that concealment is preferred where it better preserves sincerity and recipient dignity, not because visible giving is inherently void.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:272 confirms that concealed giving grants no jurisdiction over recipient guidance, and 2:273 shows why concealed need instead demands perceptive attention from the giver.

## 2:272 — ۞ لَّيْسَ عَلَيْكَ هُدَىٰهُمْ وَلَٰكِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يَهْدِى مَن يَشَآءُ ۗ وَمَا تُنفِقُوا۟ مِنْ خَيْرٍۢ فَلِأَنفُسِكُمْ ۚ وَمَا تُنفِقُونَ إِلَّا ٱبْتِغَآءَ وَجْهِ ٱللَّهِ ۚ وَمَا تُنفِقُوا۟ مِنْ خَيْرٍۢ يُوَفَّ إِلَيْكُمْ وَأَنتُمْ لَا تُظْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The recipient's guidance is removed from the giver's jurisdiction, so beneficent expenditure cannot be conditioned on ideological control.** `ه د ي B002` assigns the intended course to God, repeated `ن ف ق B002` sends out `خ ي ر B005`, beneficence, and `ب غ ي B001` makes its object `و ج ه B001/B002`, God's face and orientation. `و ف ي B001` promises full return without shortfall. Following hidden charity in 2:271 and preceding poor people whose restraint conceals need in 2:273, I infer that the giver's task is faithful direction of the gift, not ownership of the recipient's inward destination.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:273 confirms recipient autonomy through poor people whose restraint conceals their condition, while 2:274 gathers both secret and open giving as valid modes.

## 2:273 — لِلْفُقَرَآءِ ٱلَّذِينَ أُحْصِرُوا۟ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ لَا يَسْتَطِيعُونَ ضَرْبًۭا فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ يَحْسَبُهُمُ ٱلْجَاهِلُ أَغْنِيَآءَ مِنَ ٱلتَّعَفُّفِ تَعْرِفُهُم بِسِيمَٰهُمْ لَا يَسْـَٔلُونَ ٱلنَّاسَ إِلْحَافًۭا ۗ وَمَا تُنفِقُوا۟ مِنْ خَيْرٍۢ فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ بِهِۦ عَلِيمٌ

### Activated readings

1. **Quiet need requires perceptive giving because self-restraint can make confined poverty resemble sufficiency.** `ف ق ر B002` names material poverty, `ح ص ر B004` confinement on `س ب ل B001`, and `ط و ع B003` denied practical capacity for `ض ر ب B002`, setting out through the land. `ح س ب B002` makes the uninformed observer suppose `غ ن ي B001`, wealth, because `ع ف ف B001` restrains improper asking; `س و م B004` supplies distinguishing marks, while `ل ح ف B003` denies pressing insistence. I infer that charity cannot rely only on voiced demand: disciplined need transfers a duty of attentive recognition to those with resources.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:274 confirms that responsiveness to quiet need must extend across night and day and secret and open conditions, and 2:275 sharpens the contrast with extractive consumption.

## 2:274 — ٱلَّذِينَ يُنفِقُونَ أَمْوَٰلَهُم بِٱلَّيْلِ وَٱلنَّهَارِ سِرًّۭا وَعَلَانِيَةًۭ فَلَهُمْ أَجْرُهُمْ عِندَ رَبِّهِمْ وَلَا خَوْفٌ عَلَيْهِمْ وَلَا هُمْ يَحْزَنُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Mature expenditure is available across every temporal and social visibility condition.** `ن ف ق B002` releases wealth by night and by `ن ه ر B002`, opened day; `س ر ر B001` keeps some inwardly hidden while `ع ل ن B001` makes some open. `ء ج ر B001` supplies recompense and `ح ز ن B001` removes rough sadness. The fourfold pairing gathers 2:271's public and hidden modes and answers 2:273's easily missed need, before 2:275 contrasts wealth consumption through usury. I infer that no single publicity rule defines good giving; consistency of purpose across changing contexts does.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:275 makes the contrast explicit: continual outflow across every setting is followed by a form of wealth consumption that destabilizes upright standing.

## 2:275 — ٱلَّذِينَ يَأْكُلُونَ ٱلرِّبَوٰا۟ لَا يَقُومُونَ إِلَّا كَمَا يَقُومُ ٱلَّذِى يَتَخَبَّطُهُ ٱلشَّيْطَٰنُ مِنَ ٱلْمَسِّ ۚ ذَٰلِكَ بِأَنَّهُمْ قَالُوٓا۟ إِنَّمَا ٱلْبَيْعُ مِثْلُ ٱلرِّبَوٰا۟ ۗ وَأَحَلَّ ٱللَّهُ ٱلْبَيْعَ وَحَرَّمَ ٱلرِّبَوٰا۟ ۚ فَمَن جَآءَهُۥ مَوْعِظَةٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّهِۦ فَٱنتَهَىٰ فَلَهُۥ مَا سَلَفَ وَأَمْرُهُۥٓ إِلَى ٱللَّهِ ۖ وَمَنْ عَادَ فَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلنَّارِ ۖ هُمْ فِيهَا خَٰلِدُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Usurious accumulation destabilizes upright agency because it falsely equates priced exchange with guaranteed surplus.** `ء ك ل B004` makes usury consumed wealth, `ر ب و B003` its surplus increase, and `ق و م B002` upright standing disrupted by `خ ب ط B001`, irregular striking, through `م س س B004`, an affecting touch. `ب ي ع B001` is exchange for a price, but `م ث ل B001` falsely makes it the counterpart of usury; the two receive opposed legal status. `و ع ظ B001` can bring `ن ه ي B002`, cessation, preserving what `س ل ف B001`, preceded, whereas `ع و د B001/B004` makes return a repeated practice ending in `خ ل د B001`. I infer that the prohibited difference is not gain in every exchange but a debt structure that treats growth as detached from reciprocal market risk.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:276 confirms the denied equivalence through opposite outcomes, with usury erased and charity grown, while 2:278 requires abandonment of every residual claim.

## 2:276 — يَمْحَقُ ٱللَّهُ ٱلرِّبَوٰا۟ وَيُرْبِى ٱلصَّدَقَٰتِ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ لَا يُحِبُّ كُلَّ كَفَّارٍ أَثِيمٍ

### Activated readings

1. **The apparent increase of usury is removed, while relinquished charity is made actually to rise.** `م ح ق B001/B002` marks loss until removal and a waning-out, `ر ب و B003` names usurious surplus, then `ر ب و B001` reverses the root toward genuine increase for `ص د ق B006`, charity. `ح ب ب B002` denies clinging approval to persistent denial and fault. The same root's opposed branches sharpen 2:275's denied equivalence and anticipate the command to abandon remaining usury in 2:278. I infer that the passage distinguishes numerical addition from life-producing growth: one may expand the claim while eroding the economy that sustains it.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:277 embodies true growth in repaired action, worship, and alms, and 2:278 commands the creditor to leave the false surplus behind.

## 2:277 — إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَعَمِلُوا۟ ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ وَأَقَامُوا۟ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ وَءَاتَوُا۟ ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ لَهُمْ أَجْرُهُمْ عِندَ رَبِّهِمْ وَلَا خَوْفٌ عَلَيْهِمْ وَلَا هُمْ يَحْزَنُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The counter-economy joins repaired action, upright worship, and purifying growth rather than separating devotion from circulation.** `ص ل ح B003` makes deeds suitable and sound, `ق و م B002/B003` raises and undertakes `ص ل و B003`, prescribed worship, and `ز ك و B001/B002` gives alms the joined image of growth and purity. `ء ج ر B001` supplies recompense and `ح ز ن B001` is removed. Located between charity's true growth in 2:276 and the demand to leave usurious residue in 2:278, I infer that belief's economic evidence is a coordinated practice: worship and redistributed increase uphold one another.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:278-280 tests this counter-economy concretely through relinquished surplus, preserved principal, debtor respite, and superior remission.

## 2:278 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ ٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَذَرُوا۟ مَا بَقِىَ مِنَ ٱلرِّبَوٰٓا۟ إِن كُنتُم مُّؤْمِنِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Belief is tested by relinquishing not only new usury but every remaining claim generated by it.** `و ق ي B002` requires cautious self-placement, `و ذ ر B003` means leaving something be, `ب ق ي B002` identifies the residual portion, and `ر ب و B003` makes that residue usurious surplus. After 2:275 permits what preceded cessation but 2:276 exposes usury's false growth, the command targets outstanding continuation. I infer that repentance must alter the balance sheet: verbal disavowal is incomplete while the prohibited increment remains collectible.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:279 confirms that repentance must alter the balance sheet by restoring principal only, and 2:280 makes even that valid principal wait on actual capacity.

## 2:279 — فَإِن لَّمْ تَفْعَلُوا۟ فَأْذَنُوا۟ بِحَرْبٍۢ مِّنَ ٱللَّهِ وَرَسُولِهِۦ ۖ وَإِن تُبْتُمْ فَلَكُمْ رُءُوسُ أَمْوَٰلِكُمْ لَا تَظْلِمُونَ وَلَا تُظْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **The settlement line returns principal while ending both extraction and confiscation.** `ء ذ ن B003` makes continued refusal a public notification of `ح ر ب B002`, hostile opposition, from God and `ر س ل B002`, the messenger. On turning back, `ر ء س B001/B004` identifies the head or original beginning of `م و ل B001`, wealth; the paired negatives bar both inflicting and receiving wrongful deprivation. Following 2:278's command to leave residual surplus and preceding debtor respite in 2:280, I infer that abolition is precisely bounded: the creditor loses the increment but not the original capital, so anti-exploitation does not become reverse expropriation.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:280 confirms that symmetry does not require immediate enforcement: principal remains valid, but hardship governs timing and remission is better.

## 2:280 — وَإِن كَانَ ذُو عُسْرَةٍۢ فَنَظِرَةٌ إِلَىٰ مَيْسَرَةٍۢ ۚ وَأَن تَصَدَّقُوا۟ خَيْرٌۭ لَّكُمْ ۖ إِن كُنتُمْ تَعْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Even lawful principal yields temporally to insolvency, and voluntary remission exceeds delayed collection.** `ع س ر B002/B003` names straitened means and the danger of pressing an insolvent debtor; `ن ظ ر B002` grants watched respite until `ي س ر B001/B003`, ease and means. `ص د ق B006` then recasts relinquishment as charity, judged `خ ي ر B005`, more beneficent. The rule follows principal protection in 2:279 and precedes complete return and recompense in 2:281. I infer that creditor entitlement is real but not temporally absolute: the debtor's actual capacity governs enforcement, and generosity may dissolve the claim altogether.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:281 places the delayed human claim under complete final accounting, and 2:282 shows how a temporally extended debt can meanwhile be preserved by a just record.

## 2:281 — وَٱتَّقُوا۟ يَوْمًۭا تُرْجَعُونَ فِيهِ إِلَى ٱللَّهِ ۖ ثُمَّ تُوَفَّىٰ كُلُّ نَفْسٍۢ مَّا كَسَبَتْ وَهُمْ لَا يُظْلَمُونَ

### Activated readings

1. **Economic restraint is placed under a return where every self receives its acquisition in full without withholding.** `و ق ي B002` commands cautious placement toward the day of `ر ج ع B001`, return; `و ف ي B001` gives complete fullness without shortfall to what each self `ك س ب B001`, sought and obtained. The final negative can activate `ظ ل م B008`, prevention or withholding, to deny any deprivation in settlement. Following debtor respite in 2:280 and preceding documented debt in 2:282, I infer that delayed human claims and final divine accounting are not rivals: temporal mercy is possible because complete evaluation does not depend on immediate creditor enforcement.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:282 reflects final accounting in a comprehensive and just external record, while 2:283 shows that trust and inward fidelity still remain indispensable.

## 2:282 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوٓا۟ إِذَا تَدَايَنتُم بِدَيْنٍ إِلَىٰٓ أَجَلٍۢ مُّسَمًّۭى فَٱكْتُبُوهُ ۚ وَلْيَكْتُب بَّيْنَكُمْ كَاتِبٌۢ بِٱلْعَدْلِ ۚ وَلَا يَأْبَ كَاتِبٌ أَن يَكْتُبَ كَمَا عَلَّمَهُ ٱللَّهُ ۚ فَلْيَكْتُبْ وَلْيُمْلِلِ ٱلَّذِى عَلَيْهِ ٱلْحَقُّ وَلْيَتَّقِ ٱللَّهَ رَبَّهُۥ وَلَا يَبْخَسْ مِنْهُ شَيْـًۭٔا ۚ فَإِن كَانَ ٱلَّذِى عَلَيْهِ ٱلْحَقُّ سَفِيهًا أَوْ ضَعِيفًا أَوْ لَا يَسْتَطِيعُ أَن يُمِلَّ هُوَ فَلْيُمْلِلْ وَلِيُّهُۥ بِٱلْعَدْلِ ۚ وَٱسْتَشْهِدُوا۟ شَهِيدَيْنِ مِن رِّجَالِكُمْ ۖ فَإِن لَّمْ يَكُونَا رَجُلَيْنِ فَرَجُلٌۭ وَٱمْرَأَتَانِ مِمَّن تَرْضَوْنَ مِنَ ٱلشُّهَدَآءِ أَن تَضِلَّ إِحْدَىٰهُمَا فَتُذَكِّرَ إِحْدَىٰهُمَا ٱلْأُخْرَىٰ ۚ وَلَا يَأْبَ ٱلشُّهَدَآءُ إِذَا مَا دُعُوا۟ ۚ وَلَا تَسْـَٔمُوٓا۟ أَن تَكْتُبُوهُ صَغِيرًا أَوْ كَبِيرًا إِلَىٰٓ أَجَلِهِۦ ۚ ذَٰلِكُمْ أَقْسَطُ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ وَأَقْوَمُ لِلشَّهَٰدَةِ وَأَدْنَىٰٓ أَلَّا تَرْتَابُوٓا۟ ۖ إِلَّآ أَن تَكُونَ تِجَٰرَةً حَاضِرَةًۭ تُدِيرُونَهَا بَيْنَكُمْ فَلَيْسَ عَلَيْكُمْ جُنَاحٌ أَلَّا تَكْتُبُوهَا ۗ وَأَشْهِدُوٓا۟ إِذَا تَبَايَعْتُمْ ۚ وَلَا يُضَآرَّ كَاتِبٌۭ وَلَا شَهِيدٌۭ ۚ وَإِن تَفْعَلُوا۟ فَإِنَّهُۥ فُسُوقٌۢ بِكُمْ ۗ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ ۖ وَيُعَلِّمُكُمُ ٱللَّهُ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ بِكُلِّ شَىْءٍ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **Term debt is externalized into a just record whose content begins with the liable party but is protected against incapacity and diminution.** `د ي ن B003` names debt and credit, `ء ج ل B001` fixes its term, and `س م و B005` gives that term a named designation. Repeated `ك ت ب B001/B003` joins and binds the record within `ب ي ن B003`, the parties' tie, while `ع د ل B001` requires even justice. `م ل ل B004` assigns dictation to the one bearing `ح ق ق B002`, the due claim, with `ب خ س B001` barring diminution; if `س ف ه B002`, unstable judgment, `ض ع ف B001`, weakness, or absent `ط و ع B003`, capacity, prevents this, `و ل ي B003` places a guardian in charge under the same justice. I infer that documentation distributes control so neither creditor nor scribe can silently define the debt for a vulnerable obligor.

2. **Witness redundancy and comprehensive writing are designed to make testimony recoverable while remaining proportionate to transactional duration.** `ش ه د B001/B002` joins presence to knowledge-based testimony; the stated alternative uses `ر ج ل B002` and `م ر ء B001`, accepted witnesses, so if one `ض ل ل B004`, loses retention, the other `ذ ك ر B003`, restores it. `س ء م B001` bars weariness even for `ص غ ر B001`, small debts; this is more `ق س ط B001/B004`, equitable and straight-balanced, more `ق و م B008`, upright for testimony, and nearer to removing `ر ي ب B001`, suspicion. Immediate `ت ج ر B001` in `ح ض ر B001`, present trade circulating through `د و ر B003`, is excepted from writing, though `ب ي ع B001` still receives witnesses and `ض ر ر B002` bars harming scribe or witness. I infer that the gendered witness configuration is locally assigned a memory-recovery function; the wider architecture is calibrated evidentiary resilience, not a free-standing judgment about every person's cognition.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:283 supplies travel, collateral, and trust contingencies, confirming that the evidentiary system is resilient rather than mechanically uniform; 2:284 then reaches hidden contents beyond any record.

## 2:283 — ۞ وَإِن كُنتُمْ عَلَىٰ سَفَرٍۢ وَلَمْ تَجِدُوا۟ كَاتِبًۭا فَرِهَٰنٌۭ مَّقْبُوضَةٌۭ ۖ فَإِنْ أَمِنَ بَعْضُكُم بَعْضًۭا فَلْيُؤَدِّ ٱلَّذِى ٱؤْتُمِنَ أَمَٰنَتَهُۥ وَلْيَتَّقِ ٱللَّهَ رَبَّهُۥ ۗ وَلَا تَكْتُمُوا۟ ٱلشَّهَٰدَةَ ۚ وَمَن يَكْتُمْهَا فَإِنَّهُۥٓ ءَاثِمٌۭ قَلْبُهُۥ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ بِمَا تَعْمَلُونَ عَلِيمٌۭ

### Activated readings

1. **When travel removes the scribe, collateral can secure the right, but mutual trust can still replace the object if the entrusted party performs the due.** `س ف ر B003` creates the travel condition, `و ج د B001` denies finding a writer, and `ر ه ن B001` supplies security held for a right through `ق ب ض B003`, possession. The alternative repeats `ء م ن B001/B002`, settled trust and trusting confirmation, and commands `ء د ي B002`, fulfillment of what is due, with `و ق ي B002`, guarded placement. Yet `ك ت م B001` cannot conceal `ش ه د B002`, knowledge-based testimony; doing so loads `ء ث م B004` onto `ق ل ب B001`, the inward faculty. I infer that material security and personal trust are alternate supports, but neither displaces the witness's inward obligation to preserve shared truth.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:284 confirms that concealed testimony remains accountable at the level of the heart, and 2:285 turns faithful reception into heard and voiced obedience.

## 2:284 — لِّلَّهِ مَا فِى ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَمَا فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ ۗ وَإِن تُبْدُوا۟ مَا فِىٓ أَنفُسِكُمْ أَوْ تُخْفُوهُ يُحَاسِبْكُم بِهِ ٱللَّهُ ۖ فَيَغْفِرُ لِمَن يَشَآءُ وَيُعَذِّبُ مَن يَشَآءُ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ قَدِيرٌ

### Activated readings

1. **The accounting system closes by bringing both disclosed and hidden inward contents under the owner of every realm.** `ب د و B001` makes what is within visibly emerge, `خ ف ي B001` covers it, and `ح س ب B001` subjects either state to reckoning. `غ ف ر B002` can provide forgiveness, `ع ذ ب B005` punishment, and `ق د ر B003` effective capacity over every thing. The move follows 2:283's concealed testimony and heart-loaded fault, then precedes 2:285's voiced hearing and obedience. I infer that external records solve interpersonal uncertainty but cannot exhaust accountability; concealed motive and suppressed knowledge remain legible beyond the transaction's evidence system.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:285 turns the hidden-accounting warning into undivided, voiced assent, while 2:286 qualifies responsibility by capacity and petitions for pardon and aid.

## 2:285 — ءَامَنَ ٱلرَّسُولُ بِمَآ أُنزِلَ إِلَيْهِ مِن رَّبِّهِۦ وَٱلْمُؤْمِنُونَ ۚ كُلٌّ ءَامَنَ بِٱللَّهِ وَمَلَٰٓئِكَتِهِۦ وَكُتُبِهِۦ وَرُسُلِهِۦ لَا نُفَرِّقُ بَيْنَ أَحَدٍۢ مِّن رُّسُلِهِۦ ۚ وَقَالُوا۟ سَمِعْنَا وَأَطَعْنَا ۖ غُفْرَانَكَ رَبَّنَا وَإِلَيْكَ ٱلْمَصِيرُ

### Activated readings

1. **The messenger and believers answer the descended whole with undivided assent that converts hearing into willing obedience.** `ء م ن B002` repeatedly supplies trusting assent and confirmation, `ر س ل B002` identifies messenger and messengers, and `ن ز ل B002` makes the received matter sent down. `ك ت ب B001/B003` keeps the books joined and binding; `ف ر ق B001` is explicitly refused within `ب ي ن B003`, the relation among messengers. `س م ع B003` makes hearing understanding and compliance, answered by `ط و ع B001`, willing yielding; `غ ف ر B002` is requested before `ص ي ر B001/B007`, becoming toward the destination. I infer that the surah's many distinctions are not permissions to fragment their bearers: differentiated messages culminate in a single responsive posture.

### Retrospective surprises

Later 2:286 confirms that willing obedience is capacity-bounded and accompanied by petitions for pardon, mercy, and support rather than confidence in self-sufficiency.

## 2:286 — لَا يُكَلِّفُ ٱللَّهُ نَفْسًا إِلَّا وُسْعَهَا ۚ لَهَا مَا كَسَبَتْ وَعَلَيْهَا مَا ٱكْتَسَبَتْ ۗ رَبَّنَا لَا تُؤَاخِذْنَآ إِن نَّسِينَآ أَوْ أَخْطَأْنَا ۚ رَبَّنَا وَلَا تَحْمِلْ عَلَيْنَآ إِصْرًۭا كَمَا حَمَلْتَهُۥ عَلَى ٱلَّذِينَ مِن قَبْلِنَا ۚ رَبَّنَا وَلَا تُحَمِّلْنَا مَا لَا طَاقَةَ لَنَا بِهِۦ ۖ وَٱعْفُ عَنَّا وَٱغْفِرْ لَنَا وَٱرْحَمْنَآ ۚ أَنتَ مَوْلَىٰنَا فَٱنصُرْنَا عَلَى ٱلْقَوْمِ ٱلْكَٰفِرِينَ

### Activated readings

1. **Accountability is individualized by effective capacity: burden is bounded, yet each self retains the direction of what it acquires.** `ك ل ف B003` assigns undertaken obligation only within `و س ع B002`, capacity to bear, while repeated `ك س ب B001` gives the self what it seeks and obtains, for or against it. The petitions distinguish `ن س ي B001`, loss of recall, from `خ ط ء B001/B002`, missing the right and culpable error, and ask not to be taken to account through `ء خ ذ B002`. Following 2:282's accommodations for weak dictation and 2:285's willing obedience, I infer that obligation is neither erased by human limitation nor measured without it; agency is judged inside the actual room available to act.

2. **The closing prayer turns bounded responsibility into dependence on release, covering, tenderness, patronage, and aid.** `ح م ل B003/B004` names borne responsibility and burden, `ء ص ر B001/B004` a binding restraint or carried load, and `ط و ق B002` capacity under imposed burden. The requested sequence uses `ع ف و B001` to wipe fault, `غ ف ر B001/B002` to cover protectively and forgive, and `ر ح م B001` for tenderness; `و ل ي B003/B004` then names the one assuming charge in loyal support and `ن ص ر B001` aid that makes prevail. I infer that the final “our patron” is not escape from accountability but the relation that makes capacity, repair, and steadfast action possible at the surah's endpoint.

### Retrospective surprises

No later ayah exists in the clipped focus window; the retrospective reread left both closing activations intact, with bounded acquisition-accountability completed by dependence on pardon, mercy, patronage, and aid.
